Now you’re everything

Steven: So after that, I am immediately snapped into a very different state. We do the intro, like, “It’s cool,” and then just, bam. I’m in a complete non-dual aware state. So for people who don’t know what that’s like, it is like experiencing yourself as a field of awareness. Ego dissolution. You’re no longer a person sitting in a chair. There is a person sitting in a chair, but it’s not you. You are everything around you. You’re experiencing everything more or less as a field of stuff.

Now, for me, when I’m awake and doing this, it takes a while to get into this state. It’s not easy. It’s a lot of meditation. You dissolve the sort of barrier between you and the world. You’re experiencing thoughts and emotions and stuff, but there’s no real sense of self, right? But still centered on where you are, right? It takes time to get into that state. This is like, bam. All right. Now you’re everything. And not just like a regular, weak non-dual awareness. I am everything. In an instant.

So I’m just laying in my bed. There’s a vague sense of my physical body, but at this point, I’m no longer paying attention to it at all. But now I am in this black space, and stuff is there. There are things popping in, cars, trees, roads, buildings, people. And I am all of them. I don’t mean in the sense that I feel like I am everything. I could feel like they were composed out of me, like my body had extended, and I could’ve moved a tree limb like my fingers. I was everything in this space, right?

Now, all this stuff has been popped in. Again, like The Matrix. It’s just pop, here it is. It was just in an instant. Being rocketed into a state like that at the snap of your fingers is incredibly jarring. This is not the only time this has happened in my life since then, but it’s profound when it does. You’re like, “Okay, if I had any doubts before… My state has been flipped like a light switch.” They just go in and, “Let me hit that switch real quick.”

So I’m everything around me. I can feel it. And I’m experiencing all of these insights at the same time, so I’m just like, “Whoa.” And she starts talking over what’s happening. I can hear her thoughts. And she’s explaining what is happening, asking questions, like, “What is that?” Like the car or the tree. I’m like, “It’s me. It’s me. It’s all me.” We’re talking back and forth like a lecture, except the projector screen is me being projected onto my conscious awareness. All of this stuff is just being rendered onto me.

And she’s asking questions, seeing what I ask, a little back and forth. It’s like director’s commentary over a movie. It’s questions and answers. And I begin to realize this is a lecture, and she’s telling me that, “I’m going to show you how reality works from the perspective of an individual soul, because you don’t know how it works, and I can see that it’s hurting you. And I love you, and I want you to know.”

And I’m like, “Okay. Oh, okay. That’s all.” It’s just gonna point blank, “Here. Here’s how it works. You don’t have to go read through a bunch of old tomes. You don’t need to guess. Here, let me just show you directly. I’m just gonna show you. Here it is. Here’s how it works.”

And I’m like, “Ah, I’m everything.” I’m feeling it. It’s being rendered onto me, and I can tell that it’s composed out of me. And it’s incredibly profound and bizarre experiencing this. A lot of what’s happening from this point is I am processing what I’m experiencing, ’cause people are walking around, cars are doing stuff, and I’m like, “I’m all of them. I don’t have a body anymore.” I didn’t have one, but I was centered on a point before. But now I’m just all this stuff.

Joshua: Is the non-dual state extending through physical reality, from where your body is? Or is it in the imaginal space that she brought you into?

Steven: I had forgotten about my physical body at this point. It was there. If I had really shook myself, I could’ve gotten back to it. It wasn’t gone. But it was as pushed far in the background as you could, right? I wasn’t unconscious. But it was gone, gone. I was not paying attention at all. I couldn’t, ’cause what I was experiencing in this imaginal state was so much more vivid and intense. I couldn’t even think about it.

Imagine a cursory thought floats through your imagination right now while you’re in the midst of doing something really important physically. It’s like that, but inverted. Now it’s like, “Body? Huh? What is that? I don’t know what you’re talking about. Yeah, whatever. It’s over there somewhere, I think.” So that’s what’s happening.

How simple it all is

Steven: And I’m hit with this profound realization of familiarity. Like, oh my gosh, hand to forehead, like duh. Of course that’s how it is. But I don’t remember it being like this. It just feels obvious. I can feel that it’s true. Well, duh, of course that makes sense. This is the obvious answer to how everything would work. Okay, this actually makes a lot more sense now that I’m feeling it. I’m my entire reality. Okay, cool. That makes sense, ’cause I’m feeling it, right, directly.

And then I’m immediately disappointed at how simple it all is. I’m like, “Oh my God, this is how it works? I’m just everything?” That’s how it actually works, is I’m literally just everything. And it’s so simple. And it’s all just made up. I’m rendering my whole existence. I am everything I’ve ever interacted with, everything I am, everything I feel. I’m all the stuff. And I’m just projecting it onto myself. And I’m hit with disappointment and sadness. It’s so simple.

And she basically tells me, “Yep, that’s all it is. You are your entire reality. That’s all there is. From the perspective of an individual soul.” There’s caveats of, there are other things and configurations. There are other levels of existence. You’re connected to other things in other ways. We’re not getting into that. From an individual soul’s perspective, so every human being here, that’s what you should care about. This is what matters to you. This is how it is. That’s all there ever is, is you. It’s all just you. You’re always only interacting with yourself. There’s nothing else. And I’m sad. I’m immediately disappointed.

But then I just find this moment of inner courage, ’cause I’m still experiencing everything. And I just say that… No. I decide that it matters, just in that moment. It’s also pure potential. And I’m like, I decide what matters. Ultimately, this is the only choice that matters. Does anything matter? Does nothing matter or everything matter? I decide it’s a choice. They’re two sides of the same coin. There’s nothing real or everything is real. I decided that it’s real.

The people, the stuff, all these things are real, and then I say that they matter. I choose that these things matter, even though it is all just me. It’s all just made up, air quotes. It’s the Dumbledore Harry Potter quote right at the end of the series, right? Harry’s like, “Oh, this was all made up.” And he’s like, “Well, just because it was all made up, why do you think that that means it isn’t real?” That’s the rough quote.

Literally this, I’m experiencing it now, and I’m like, well, what is the other choice? Complete nihilism? Nothing matters? Who cares? The Matrix is all there is. Or I decide that, well, that’s all there is, so I’m going to enjoy it. I’m gonna find meaning in this.

Joshua: Was that the disappointment, the nihilism?

Steven: The disappointment was the simplicity of it all. That, oh, there’s nothing else. It’s just me. And I started to feel profoundly lonely as well. But at this point, my brain is burning out. I am so exhausted from this sheer amount of things I’ve experienced. But yeah, it came from knowing that it’s all just this experience. It’s just me. I’m the only one here. I’m always the only one here. I’m just sock puppets with myself.

Awareness precedes control

Steven: And then I start to have a lot of questions. I’ve moved past the moment of nihilism, of, dang, okay, well, it’s that simple. It’s literally just all me having the experience with myself. I move past that ’cause then I wanna know stuff. I’m like, well, wait a minute. Why am I not like this all the time? I’m gonna wake up in a little bit or get up, and I know this is gonna end, and I’m probably gonna go back to a regular existence. Why am I not like this all the time? Why can’t I control it? If it’s all me, why can’t I do whatever I want?

And she responds with, “Because you weren’t aware of it. It is this way, you just didn’t realize it, and you have to realize it in order to control it.” So awareness precedes control. It’s a very common lesson in all this stuff. You have to be aware of it, and not just in a conceptual way, like I know that it is true, like you heard about it and you believe it, but you’re experiencing it. You are experiencing the tree as yourself. Then you can do whatever you want.

This is a preview of where I needed to go, and I knew that intuitively. Oh, I’m going to experience something like this in my waking life at some point. And when I get to that stage, that’s how you can fly around ignoring space-time in a UFO. That’s how you can do whatever you want, because you’re literally just controlling your own reality, like pfft, I do whatever I want.

The rules, they’re all soft rules. There are rules. There are physics, there are metaphysics, there’s friction, there’s energy… All of these things do stuff. There are rules. Every rule is a soft rule, and if you have sufficient awareness, direct awareness, you can control anything. It’s your reality. It’s you. It’s always just you. Every single soul has their own reality, right? So this is what I’m getting in these interactions.

And I wanna know, okay, sure. I just gotta keep going. I gotta get to this. I need to relive this awareness that I’m experiencing now in this imagined, like projectors, this matrix, this simulation you dumped me in to show me what it will feel like. I need to get to this point in my waking life later. Cool, I’m on board for that. But how do I do this? I don’t know how to be a tree. I don’t know how cells work. I don’t know how a combustion engine is put together. I didn’t imagine all the parts of this building.

The shared library

Steven: And so she shows me, you imagine you wanted to go to a building to do something, or a D&D campaign sort of thing. I’m gonna go do this thing, and I see a building and stuff. You didn’t create the pipes. You didn’t create the wiring, but all of those things will be there because your intention was to go to a building for a purpose. And they will appear in your consciousness, and you do not need to know how to consciously do it.

Hand wave, this is the magic of the universe, right? Or God, or however you wanna frame it, right? There was definitely a hand wave to something greater beyond me. Yes, I am my own reality, but there is a lot more than you, son. And don’t go thinking that there is no anything bigger beyond you. There definitely is. And one of those things is this shared library of where all this stuff comes from. Pipes, tools, people, stories, books, catchphrases, songs. Everything that has ever been created goes into this library, like a blueprint library.

And I got this sense of, it is profoundly, I don’t know, holy. This person did seem to have a vague sort of religion, but it was not like any of our religions. There was this profound sense of holiness in creating new things, and that by living, physical people living, anybody doing stuff, they were contributing to this shared library from which any of us can pull from.

When we’re creating our own realities, we can only create things that exist. And so we either have to create it or we have to take it from our forebears. It’s a beautiful inheritance of all this stuff that has been made. And the beauty of our souls, our consciousness, it just knows how to put this together to give us what we want. So we only ever have to focus on what we actually want, and the rest just boop, boop, boop, boop puts itself together according to our needs. If you had complete control, right? This is what I’m experiencing.

A multiplayer game

Steven: And then the last question I had, because I’m tiring out at this point, is how are there other people? I know there are other people and they’re real. You’re real. I’m interacting with you. This isn’t all me. She’s like, “Nope. This is all you. Even your experience of me is you.” Again, like a puppet. But I know there are… This is a multiplayer game. She’s like, “It is, but we don’t… You’re…” She starts to talk about it, and I have to stop her.

And I’m like, “I am so excited. I am absolutely thrilled. I wanna know this more than I wanna know anything, but you have given me so much.” I just learned that I’m my entire reality. A bunch of other stuff has happened to me. You’re real, and I’m feeling it, right? You didn’t tell me, you showed me. I felt what it was like. And all of this other stuff that I just talked about, and my brain is absolutely melting. I am so hot, I feel like I’ve had a fever. I am melting down as a human being. The physical body is now like, “Uh-oh.” Critical meltdown.

“You cannot tell me a single more thing.” That’s basically the exact quote. “You cannot say one more thing to me or I am going to have a psychotic break or my brain is going to explode. You have got to stop. No more.” And then she just smiles and basically says she loves me and says, “Okay.” And then it’s gone. I’m awake in my bed instantly, just like that. It’s completely gone. I’m just laying there like, “Ugh.” Looking at the ceiling now, like, “Whoa! Okay. Hello. I’m no longer everything in this simulation. I’m this guy in a bed.” And that’s it. And I’m just laying there. So that’s the end of that experience.

Joshua: Wow. I only heard parts of that, I think, before, so I was just riveted. I don’t even know where to begin with diving deeper.

Steven: Wherever you want, man.

Joshua: The library things, I’ve heard of the Akashic Record, and people talk about that, and other spiritual explorers have been to those spaces. It’s just interesting to think about that and how lived experience then is kind of this process of generating more that goes into this vast store. And then I also thought it was interesting about the sock puppets. So you didn’t really get a response on that. You said, “I’m talking to someone,” but it was like, no, you’re talking to yourself. But so she’s also you. I guess in that sense, you’re me and I’m you, so I’m just talking to myself and talking to you.

Steven: Yeah, man.

Joshua: But I guess you also had another experience which was Reality 102, and that’s the multiplayer reality, right?

Steven: Yeah. So spoiler… This is at the end of December, right? So this is five months ago now. A little more than five, five and a half almost. And for four months and some change maybe, I’ve been begging. “Yo, give me the follow-up. I want the 102.” Nope. Nothing. Nothing. Now, an incredible amount of things happened to me in that time period, but no follow-up, no direct instruction on this topic. Other topics, which is maddening, but not like this. Not to this fidelity.

And I’d been begging. It bothered me. How am I not alone? How are we each not alone? I know these other people are real. And the only thing I had gotten was an intuitive sense that we were projecting onto each other. And I’d only been given the sense that it only happens because you don’t know about it, right?

This was another thing that was given to me in part of the lecture near the end, was, I’m not making this happen, so how is it happening to me? There’s another person there. How does this happen? And that this can only happen to you, anything can only happen to you because you’re not aware that it’s you. So you’re just at the mercy of whatever’s going on, buddy.

You’re gonna receive, you’re the screen, man. You can’t stop anything. Not really. You have some control, but you don’t have this level of awareness that I’m basically giving you right now. So you can’t shut it down, because you’re not aware that it’s you. If you knew that it was you and that you were your entire reality, you could control anything. You could literally do anything. You could have any experience. You could do anything you wanted, but it would be you.

And this was the part that I got hung up on ’cause I’d never got the answer in this experience. In the 101, it’s like, okay, I get that, but it’s still just me. You’re like a bubble person where people are reaching in with their gloves and touching you. Maybe. I don’t know. How can I tell the difference? It’s all me. I’m interacting with myself everywhere, all the time. And I wanted to know how, but I didn’t get that until a month ago. So there were follow-ups to this. Stay tuned. That’ll be another episode. But for now, there you have it.

Keep making stuff

Steven: And for the creatives out there, keep making stuff because now you know, if you ever doubt that something you made had any value at all, in a lot of ways, as far as other people are concerned, this is one of the only things that have enduring permanent value, is your book or your music or your ideas or the thing you put together, because it’s automatically made available to somebody else, whether they knew it or not. Your inventions, your concepts, all of this, your poetry, anything can be made available to somebody else once it’s been created.

They may think it’s theirs. I mean, don’t begrudge somebody in their own dimension or whatever for thinking they came up with the idea, but you will know that it was yours, and you made that possible for them. This, in a lot of ways, is the only way we can ever make progress, is by creating this stuff. So nobody’s life is ever wasted. You’ve always made some contribution.

And creative acts are, as it felt, ultimately a beautiful, benevolent, tantamount to a holy act, is what it felt like. With reverence, right? It was treated with reverence, not silliness. Because you’ve created something that will last for eternity. For everybody, for all of the universe, every living soul can have it now.

Joshua: That makes me think of, going back to what I was sharing earlier about my own creative journey, I used to think that ideas came from my own head, or that if I strained enough, then the good words would come out or the good stories would come out. But the more that I learned to let go and release control and become a channel, I felt I was tapping into something beyond me. And I’d read this book called Big Magic by Elizabeth Gilbert, and she says that she had an experience where she was working for years on a novel, and at some point her life got beyond her and she couldn’t keep working on this project anymore.

And then a couple of years later, she met another novelist friend of hers who had ended up writing a book that was almost exactly the same book. It was the same characters, the same setting, the same major conflicts and everything. And maybe it was slightly different in significant ways, but it was like, wait, that was the book I was writing. So somehow this novel went from Elizabeth Gilbert’s head into, I think it was Ann Patchett maybe. I don’t know who the other novelist was. She ended up writing it. So that novel Elizabeth Gilbert was writing didn’t go to waste.

Steven: Nothing is wasted.

Joshua: So my idea is that there’s a story that’s existing in a non-physical realm that’s trying to come through me into this physical realm, and then I’m availing myself as a channel to help that happen. But it’s maybe working with all these other people. The effort that I make to bring it forth is also helping them. I also think about Darwin and Wallace, right? They both were working on the theory of evolution in different parts of the world without knowing of each other’s work. But Darwin was the one who popularized his idea first, so he’s the one who’s credited for it. So we don’t really actually own those things in the way that copyright law and publishers would wanna say that we do.

Steven: So that’s coming from the other direction. That’s ideas coming into your world for you to work on them or ’cause it’s time for them to show up or whatever, right? It’s a two-way street. That’s pretty cool. I totally buy that as well.

Taking it seriously

Joshua: So I’m wondering now, having shared the story, brought it into awareness and shared it in this space, do you have any reflections on, or meaning you’re making of that now?

Steven: Well, it’s hard not to leak in some of the stuff I’ve experienced since then. But just as a spoiler, I have had this experience, or one very like it now, in a waking state, which I did not see coming. I should have. In the telling of the story, it seems like it all fits together sequentially, but it’s such a mess. There’s 50 things going on all the time. You don’t know which is real, which is made up, which is, I think there’s something. And then suddenly, bam, a feeling, what was an inclination or an intuition…

I took my intuition and imagination and all these inner senses very seriously to get to this point, and then suddenly, bam, you get hit with this high fidelity, low error experience that you can’t ignore. It’s in your face. And I got validation after this, by the way. I didn’t have time to get into that, but I would later see things and get physical world confirmation that this had happened, just as a courtesy, I think. For my own sanity. Because the doubt is such a pervasive thing. How can you imagine doubting after this? But that’s what happens.

But I took these inner senses seriously long enough for them to show up in my world. That’s really what happened, is that I was willing to play in this sort of maybe space of taking it seriously, like, no, these emotions, these are people. This is happening, somewhere. I’m not getting it all right, I know that. There’s a lot of noise, but I know there’s something here. I took it seriously long enough, then bam. You get hit with, oh, okay, you’re right in front of me. This is for real, for real.

And you’re just telling me all of this and it all comports and it’s testable. You’re getting models that fit, and you can try things, or you’re getting advice or definitions to things that you can test in the real world. And that started happening. But it came from being willing to entertain a thing that didn’t feel real for a long time, as it gradually became more real. It was incredibly frustrating, and that’s what made it happen, and if you can hold onto that long enough…

But there was also accountability, right? I insisted on validation. I insisted on doing it right, and a lot of the stuff that I experienced that was traumatic was made up. Just hearing thoughts or ideas, you imagine somebody saying they hate you after all this time. What do you mean you hate me? What is this? And a lot of those things didn’t happen, but they did kind of happen. We’ll get into that later too, because it’s your reality, right?

Your insecurities, your fears, your hopes, your dreams, even especially the dark subconscious stuff that you don’t wanna pretend is there, that’s all deeply influencing the very real physical reality you see in front of you. It’s changing what is possible and what will happen to you. The physical world is not as malleable as this astral place, but it runs by the same principles ultimately. Maybe at a slower cadence, generally speaking, more ordered, but it fundamentally works in the same way, right? Just think of it as having more mass. It’s harder to get stuff moving. But it works on the same principles. So all of these parts of yourself are also creating the world, and you’re experiencing them too.

It’s always you

Steven: So as I went through this, and anyone else, just treat your non-physical experiences very seriously. They are happening. But it’s always you. It’s always you. Everything is always you, and that is extremely confusing and profound and simple. We’ll get into obviously later, how is it not always you? It’s clearly, there are other people and stuff here. But this is true, full stop. You’re always only interacting with yourself. And a lot of that comes from your unconscious parts.

And so I think the final point I wanted to make about all this experience was that the way all of this happened to me was personal growth. It didn’t happen because I went on a drug bender, or I learned some secret technique, or some magic spell, got hypnotized, or it didn’t happen because I was at some horrible point in my life. I had been better than I ever was. I’d been on a trajectory of personal growth for years, and I was taking spiritual and non-physical stuff very seriously, ’cause it started happening to me. I didn’t ask for it. It just started showing up. And the more seriously I took it, the more it happened.

But all of this stuff, even this experience, I was looking through my journals. Immediately preceding this were moments of profound personal growth where I’d accepted parts of myself that I didn’t like, I didn’t wanna admit. And then I’d accepted them, and I was beginning to move on, or relationships with other people, or about what I really wanted or what I actually feared, I dragged that stuff out into the light and accepted it. And that had happened right before this.

And when I look back at a lot of the major experiences, they always happen following periods of profound personal growth. That’s how you get psychic powers and see UFOs and meet your soul family, is become a better, happier person. More open, more accepting and self-loving, less judgmental. That is what created this experience just as much as taking it seriously and wanting it. I was seeking this for a really long time. You do those three things, you can have the same experience.

But yeah, it’s always you. Just because it feels imaginary doesn’t mean it’s fake, because it’s all that. It’s all you. “Oh, this is just me,” is always true. Yes, it is just me. And so that was one of the biggest takeaways from that, and it took a long time for that to sink in, and I needed a lot more personal examples. This isn’t a thing you can be told. Being told just helps you take it seriously and put yourself on the path to go experience it yourself. Everyone needs to experience it yourself, and you can, and then you will know. You’ll put your hand to your forehead and go, “Oh my God, it really is just me.” That’s available to anyone.

The gift of everything

Joshua: Wow. I’m feeling encouraged and excited to try out some of these things myself. I’m already doing some of that and getting little glimpses of some of the things that you’re talking about in some of my liminal states and meditations. But yeah, just honored to hold space for your story today and to be able to create this series with you. How does it feel to have shared this story in this space today?

Steven: It feels amazing, like I’ve offloaded the first chapter of one of these golden books. Honestly, it’s a weight. It’s like a beautiful burden. But I feel like I’m walking around these days knowing I am my entire reality. I feel it. I can feel it. And I can start to do stuff. I can see without eyeballs. I can move things with my mind. Light things. But I can do things now. I can see and know things that I shouldn’t theoretically be able to know. And it feels amazing, and I want everyone else to have it. So I’m just excited to get it out and get it into people’s hands, like you and like anyone else, so they can have it too.

Joshua: This is Reality 101.

Steven: We thought this through about what would be a good sign-off, and I’ll give this context one time, and the next I’m just gonna say it. At one point in one of my experiences through a remote viewing map, somebody told me that they were so excited for me because I had just been given the gift of everything. In fact, everyone has already received this gift. They just didn’t realize it yet. This is prior to this experience. You have received the gift of everything. Surprise. And here’s what the gift of everything is. You can do anything you want. Nothing is impossible. You’re not going to die, and everything loves you. There it is. See you next time.

See, Wake Up, Remember: Part One

Episode 2

Jeff Cameron · before you read

The next three chapters are one long conversation, released over two months, about what happened between the first lecture and the second. They are the part of the series where he is least sure of himself and most honest about it. Early on he warns Joshua that he tells it as a clean path so that it can be followed, and that living it was nothing like that.

This first part covers the days right after: the drive home, an offer to answer five questions, a ball of light on a night walk. Then it turns practical, to the psi wheel and to seeing with his eyes closed, and Joshua tests him on the spot by holding colored pens up to a camera. Read that test for what it is. It is informal, the lighting is bad, the results are mixed, and it settles nothing. He agrees to it without hesitating, which tells you something about him.

Joshua: This episode of Reality 101 is called See, Wake Up, Remember. We’ll explore how to know what is real, how to make it real, and what real even means, if anything. So before we dive into that, we always like to do a little check-in in the beginning, so just wondering how you’re arriving today, Steven.

Steven: I’m good. I just went for a run. I took a little nap, had a pretty good meditation. I always look through my journals before we do these recordings, so I’m often going to arrive in the same place, which is, holy crap, a lot has happened to me. You wouldn’t think that you would forget, but my mind is a sieve, and it’s like I have to load it back up into memory in order to even tell the story because it all leaves me.

I’m sure it’s all baked in there somewhere, and I remember the peak moments. But I always come in in a pretty jovial mood, just shocked at what I’ve been through, and excited. So I’m feeling pretty good. I’m about to go on vacation, so I’m feeling even better because of that.

Joshua: Where are you going?

Steven: I’m going on a meditation retreat, Darren King’s meditation retreat.

Joshua: I wish I could’ve made it to that. I’ve been in his classes and really enjoyed that community.

The guinea pig

Joshua: Well, I’m arriving today feeling excited. It was cool to record our last episode, and just eager to hear more of your experiences. It’s really been encouraging to hear about your practices and the experiences that have resulted from those, and the revelations that you’ve come to through that. I’ve been doing more of those myself since our last recording.

Steven: You mind if I ask what specifically?

Joshua: I’m already doing a lot of meditation, deep meditation, and you’ve shared some guided meditations with me that have been useful. And just touch points, even little parts of seeing reality differently, and wondering into that. Like you shared about non-dual experience you had recently. And so as I’m walking around, or just in meditation, I stretch my mind, my imagination into that space and feeling into that possibility.

And I’ve been having these experiences where I feel as if I’m awakened in the early hours around 3:00 a.m. Many people who have been doing meditation for a long time and are on a spiritual path know that is a prime time to meditate and to have deep experiences. I’ve had many altered state experiences with psychedelics, and 3:00 a.m. I would say is a kind of altered state too, where everything is really quiet. My mind is quiet. The world is quiet. Many say that there’s a quiet in the psychic space too, as everyone else around you is sleeping.

Steven: It’s prime time, man.

Joshua: So that’s when I have really deep experiences, anomalous encounters. I won’t put a label on these. And it fits really well into the topic of this episode because these experiences feel more real than real. My perception is that I am contacting something or something is in contact with me. I’ll be sitting in meditation, and all of a sudden my body will light up with this kind of tingling sensation from the core. All my hair is standing on end, just like a vibrational sort of state.

And last time you shared about meeting your soul mother, and I’ve been making this intention since our meeting, so that’s another one of the practices, the power of intention, to shape your experience and shape your reality. I’ve been making this intention. I want to participate in greater reality, which is something I learned from you. And then I was like, “I want to meet my soul family. I want to meet my soul mother.”

And so I was sitting in meditation. I get one of these pings. In the past, I’ve gotten them with intentionally reaching out to different beings or presences. I’ve gotten pings when I reached out to the Earth and felt Earth awareness, and reaching out to the sun or my ancestors, or I have a relationship with Jesus as well as one of my guides. And all of those, I felt more intentional reaching out. But this felt as if something was reaching out to me, ’cause the ping just came as I was meditating, and it brought my awareness down to my heart.

And then all of a sudden this ping comes, and I was like, “Oh, who is there?” And then the thought comes, “Soul Mother?” And then, as if in response, there’s a bigger ping, and this experience proceeds along the lines similar to what you described in our last conversation. Feels as if the dialogue is happening, where I’m asking a question and the response comes, but it’s my own thought.

So I’m thinking, “Is this happening in my own head? Am I making this all up?” And I’m just allowing myself to believe into that possibility that I really am having a conversation with something, and maybe these responses are real, are coming from somewhere non-physical, some being, Soul Mother.

So it progressed along those lines. Steven and I are both writers, we’ve both written a lot of dialogue, and it kind of feels like that. When you start a conversation, the first person to speak elicits a particular response from the next person to speak. Steven: I needed it, right? People would call this a white light orb UFO. I would call this a soul manifested physically, right? I would now. I didn’t know that before, but she was talking to me, so it was pretty obvious that that’s what that was. It was big. It wasn’t like a little, tiny ghost orb. This was, ooh, this is luminous. This thing’s bright. And it came from, as you mentioned, following something that was definitely not physical, a feeling, a thought, and I followed these breadcrumbs to the point where eventually I got profound experiences, which you’re starting to have, but those are still felt, right? Those are subjective. They’re just an experience, air quotes.

You will eventually always get validation, even physical world validation, and you should expect it, but you’re gonna have to be very patient, and you’re gonna have to sit with what everyone else will think is a stupid idea long enough to see that it is true. So this is middle road where you take it seriously and you follow it, knowing that you have to hold it lightly because many of these things won’t pan out, or you’ll be wrong in a way that teaches you something about yourself. I was just looking at myself, and that’s okay. As you now know, you’re always looking at yourself.

And so there’s this push-pull patience. Like, okay, I opened my eyes and I saw nothing. When I was laying in bed, I’m like, “Oh, pfft, it’s all fake.” You can’t rubber band this hard or you’ll kill it. You’ll crush it. You also can’t become so obsessive that you become delusional, because now you’re detached from an actual signal, right? You’re just pumping your own signal at yourself. You’re living your own delusion now. You’re not actually communicating with anyone or anything.

So you should expect accountability and eventual validation, but you’re gonna have to be way more patient than you ever wanted to be for the good stuff. Not for the cool experiences. Those can start happening, but for physical world validation, it takes way longer than you want it to. But it does eventually happen because it’s true. It’s the same principles. It’s the same place. There’s no difference. The spiritual world or whatever is not separate.

The psi wheel

Joshua: So what came to mind is your practice of spinning the psi wheel. A psi wheel is a little… I’ll show you mine right here. It’s a sponge, and there’s a needle, and there’s a little bit of aluminum foil on top.

Steven: It’s like a little tent. Hold it over the top. It spins on it ’cause there’s low friction.

Joshua: You’ve shared with me about your practice of learning to spin this thing and have shared videos of you accomplishing this. I’m still on the fence whether those are AI generated.

Steven: Oh, come on.

Joshua: No, I’m just kidding. I believe you. But you said that you had to sit for 30 hours just staring at this thing, hoping and believing that it’s possible before you could even get it to spin a little bit. And how challenging that must be, and I’ve done a little bit of that, just beginning to step into that practice. The thought is just like, “This is stupid. Is this possible?” I don’t know at what point will it shift, but I feel like that’s the thought that comes up. I love that phrase you mentioned again just now, and I think you mentioned it last episode. You have to be willing to sit, believe something stupid for long enough that it becomes true.

Steven: That’s exactly how it works. So I had had the benefit of other forms of physical validation. So at this point, I can see without my eyeballs. I can close my eyes. I can see. I was able to see out of the back of my head for a little while. I stopped training that skill because it’s really disorienting. Now that proves it to me, right? I can prove it to other people. If you were to put me in front of an optometrist or whatever, we could set it up so that they would know for sure that, yeah, his eyelids are closed. He can’t see this. I haven’t bothered to engineer that test because I don’t care. I’m not doing this to prove it to anyone else. I’m doing it for me, right? I know, and that’s enough. I saw through the mask. It’s good enough for me.

But I’d also been doing telekinesis practice, so the ability to move things, in this case, a very light thing, without touching it. At that time, I would’ve said I was using my mind to move it, but now, given the knowledge in Reality 101, I’m moving myself, right? That’s how psi works. That’s how psychic powers work, is, oh, wow, I can see. That’s so amazing. You’re just looking at yourself. Of course you can. How you can move stuff with your mind, sort of. You can move yourself. Like, duh. But there are rules in there of physics, and there’s resistance. A lot of it is mental, but not all.

So I didn’t know anyone that knew telekinesis. This is not my world. I am coming from software development. I was an atheist. This is so far outside my understanding, but I’m obsessively exploring everything. I read a book on telekinesis by Sean McNamara called The Telekinesis Practice Method. I just happened to see it on Amazon. I don’t even know what led me to it. I’m like, “I’m gonna buy that.” Why? I don’t know why. I would’ve thought that was stupid, but I did it and I read it. I didn’t know anyone who knew how to do this.

So I read the book, and I set up this wheel, and I practiced over the course of three to six months, at least 30 hours total time staring at this thing. It becomes like one of my regular meditations, 30 minutes or so at a time, up to an hour sometimes, depending how obsessive I am, just staring at this stupid piece of aluminum foil. Move. I try everything. Everything. Everything you can imagine, and it doesn’t move, and it’s so infuriating. At this point, I don’t have any mindsight. I don’t even think I was doing much telepathy or anything then. I just had this sense that it should be possible. I’d already seen UFOs. I’m like, “Man, if that’s possible, this has gotta be…” I don’t wanna give up.

And then one day, I’m staring at this piece of aluminum foil, and I just have this feeling like it’s gonna move. I know it is. I can feel that it is. It’s going to. And then it wiggles, and then it starts to turn, and I just start crying. I’m openly crying. It’s like, “I did it.” Like I said, without touching it. I’m three feet away from this thing. It’s in a shoebox, basically, in a recessed cubby in my walk-in closet, where there’s no airflow. So I know when this thing moves and doesn’t move. It wasn’t vibration. It was nothing. I’ve been staring at this thing for 30 hours of my life. I know what makes it move and doesn’t make it move, intimately.

And it spins, and I start to cry because it’s real. My experiences aren’t things that are just happening to me now at that point. And it’s like, I can do it. I needed to see something physical move.

And since then, as you well know, I’ve done maybe close to 100 hours of practice in telekinesis now, and I can make it move under glass, which doesn’t sound that fancy, under a big bell glass jar. So this thing is isolated. That doesn’t sound hard, but it’s at least 10 times harder. And I can do it from a distance as well. I don’t even have to touch it now. It’s hard, but I can do it maybe one in two sessions, one in three sessions. I can do it without touching it from a few feet away under a bell jar. I can get it to spin now, and that’s practice, so anyone can do it.

But I needed to see physical validation. How much it matters, I think especially to the human brain. You need to see something in your physical environment ’cause it’s what we understand. It’s what we’re used to. And that changed me for sure.

Joshua: Wow. I can only get mine to move when the window’s open and there’s a breeze coming through.

Steven: You’ll get it. You’ll get it. Start with your hands.

Joshua: I’m like, “Oh, it moved,” and I realized, oh, I’m breathing too hard, or, oh, the window’s open.

Steven: Yeah. It’s like that.

What’s the point

Joshua: What’s the point of cultivating these psi capacities?

Steven: So this is my personal theory, right? And I think we’ll talk about this when we get into mindsight. This is a whole part of the story arc that’s just been totally ignored. It was so profound. But I’ve noticed this. It is every time you have a big breakthrough in some psychic ability. So at this point, I’d done remote viewing practice. I’d done telekinesis, and this was before mindsight. I was doing other stuff, right? Anytime you get a big breakthrough, like, whoa, that just worked, there’s this surge of energy, and I mentioned it to Kate, our mutual friend, before.

It’s a lot like Stormlight Archives when somebody says the words of power and they ascend to the next level of their Knight Radiant abilities. There’s this outburst of power, like fusion, like atoms or like fission being split for the first time, and it blows you wide open. And experiences, without fail, that night, the first time I did it with glass, the first time I did it ever, big experiences right afterward. Opening my mindsight for the first time, woof, wild experiences. At night, even waking state, in meditation, just all sorts of incredibly vivid things, profound feelings and information. It’s like it just blows open the aperture.

So that is one reason, is that developing psychic capacities definitely has a functional connectivity between you and the other side or whatever, non-material reality. It blows open those doors. Now, that effect is short-lived. You get a huge burst, right? But once it comes back, your baseline’s higher. You’re more sensitive now. It’s opening you up.

But that’s only one of the things it does. I think it also trains your belief. If I ever needed to know that reality is fundamentally not just physical, I just do this. Like, yeah, okay. Yeah, that’s my hand. I have my eyes closed. I can see it. Yeah, okay.

All of this stems from belief, imagination, intent. That’s where it all comes from. That is what opens the aperture to sensitivity, to making these things happen, is that you believe it can, and you believe it’s possible for you, and you want it. You’re feeding this like a little Tamagotchi. And if you just keep feeding it, it slowly opens up these apertures to be more sensitive to these things and to let it come into your world. That’s what you’re doing.

And so I think psychic powers, seeing them happen, believing you can do it, actually doing it, and going, “Holy shit, you can move things without touching them. I can see without eyes. I can learn things about the world that I’m not physically present for.” Once you do that, it changes you. It changes what you expect, and what you expect changes what you believe next, and that creates this stairstep. So I think it’s an integral part of advancing spiritual development.

And personal development, of course, is key as well, but I think that this also acts as a multivitamin against cultural pressure and familial pressure of friends who are constantly cooking you. They’re pasteurizing your beliefs. This is impossible. This is impossible. You wanna see a piece of tinfoil stop spinning or to stop seeing without your eyeballs, you just get somebody in the room who thinks that you’re crazy, or they’re judging. They think you’re dumb. Turns out like a light. This stuff just stops immediately, until you develop a sense of inner confidence.

I can see with Mindsight now even if somebody’s screaming in my face that I’m an idiot and that it doesn’t work. But for the first few months, absolutely not. Even my own doubt, you can see it in real time. It’s hilarious. You’re, “Wait, am I actually, or am I peeking somehow?” I’m squinting through my eyes. Bam. It’s off. And you’re like, “Oh, I’m so sorry. Please come back. No, no, no.”

So if you ever doubted whether your intention or your beliefs are affecting your reality, psychic powers end up being a really good, I guess, bellwether. Just a way to test, how open am I? How permissive am I feeling? How am I allowing things in? Because that tinfoil ain’t gonna spin if you don’t believe it. And so if it happens immediately, you get that real-world validation. So it also ends up being a really good barometer for, how connected am I? How tuned in am I? And it won’t lie.

You shouldn’t use that as your only test, of course, because you’re gonna become demotivated. And other things can go wrong, right? There are other factors. If it spins, you know that you have at least some capacity here. So it ends up being a decent benchmark and a way to practice. So all of those are good reasons to do it. Also, it’s awesome. I think that’s the number one reason, right? We can.

What matters most

Joshua: There’s a lot of really vibrant threads that I’m excited to pull. So I’m wondering if you would like to share some reflections, having shared this story. Any meaning you’re making of this, or new perspectives you’re gaining from sharing it now in this space?

Steven: It’s fun for me. So we got through the personal story. We’ll get to the rest in part two of this episode. This is my life. These are people to me. This isn’t just a fun story. I am in a unique position. Told my other friend Tony that every time I tell these stories, I know they’re there. I know they can listen to me. Every now and then, you get a little impression, for the most part, but this is my life. These are people to me. This is like a family. It’s not just a thing I trot out because it’s, “Ha ha, check out this cool story I’ve got.” It matters on a very deep level to me. I know it is true. I haven’t just had some fun experiences. It hasn’t stopped, right?

And I was wrong about a lot of the impressions along the way. But just like the coin thing, it always ends up converging on the truth if you’re patient enough. If you just keep getting back up and trying again, you’re willing to admit, “Oh, I was wrong about that,” getting some conflicting impressions. If you’re patient enough and you keep following it and you never stop believing that there is something here, but you also hold it lightly, you don’t need the right answers right away. You don’t get the validation right away. If you can sit with it and keep going, it does turn into something.

And what it turned into for me was what matters most to me. You saw it in the questions that I asked. Just loving relationships, just finding out that somebody had been there, multiple somebodies had been there the whole time, my entire life, and how touching that is, and that that is true to some degree in different ways. Everyone has their own story like this. You just didn’t know it. You’re sitting here going to work, you’re bored, you feel like your life has no meaning or things are pointless. Meanwhile, somebody there cares about you. They’re watching you. They’re there, and they care about you, and they want things for you, and they influence you, and you’re not alone.

And there’s nothing bad or sinister or unnerving really about it other than the realization that you are loved, and you’re not alone, and you never were. You just thought you were. That is how it feels to tell this story, is how profound that is, and that’s still what matters most to me. As crazy as it sounds, getting all of this information about how reality works and being able to do cool psychic stuff, you would think that those are the highlights, and they’re awesome.

But ultimately, at the end of the day, at least for me, and hopefully for a lot of other people, it’s finding out who’s out there that loves you and what they want for you, and that they’ve cared about you this whole time, and that they’ve been helping you this whole time. And to me, that is what matters most, and it still is. I think there’s something beautiful about that.

Joshua: I feel honored to hear the story more in depth and to have these conversations. They always sit with me in my mind, and I like this idea of, you mentioned shatterings, a series of shatterings, and how every time who I think I was and where I thought I was going breaks open, something truer comes through, and a version of myself that lives a fuller life. And it’s kind of incredible that this is where I find myself now. Steven: There’s a lot of work to be done to get to that point, to be fair. You need to be able to keep a job and put your clothes on and not have anger management problems or drug addiction problems. There’s a lot of work to get to the top of Maslow’s hierarchy. It’s not a simple task. It’s a tough world that we find ourselves in. But you can make it.

See, Wake Up, Remember: Part Two

Episode 3

Jeff Cameron · before you read

In the episode’s description he wrote that this one is “about how it feels to work, alone, for something impossible, not knowing whether you’ll ever get it.”

It opens in January, back at his job, depressed, with the experiences gone. There is a fox, and his late father’s voice on the night before his birthday. Then two months of effort with nothing to show for it. It ends on April 8th, in a closet and then in a hammock, with the experience he says he made the show to describe.

If you only know the confident voice of the first lecture, read this one for the other voice.

Steven: Yeah. I remember what we talked about. I mean, you literally brought it up, what we talked about last time. Nothing is off limits

It always starts so small

But she was saying, “Just keep going.” Almost like your child is distraught. “Sorry you’re struggling,” but it always feels like imagination. It always starts in imagination. That’s how it starts. You have to believe it first, and it starts so small, but you just have to let it grow.

I know it starts feeling imaginary, but that’s how it always happens. You have to let that happen in order to get to a point where it’s not just imaginary. It’s happening in the rest of your life, too. But basically, you’re never gonna get there if you keep stomping on it. You’re gonna stay where you are. And so it’s these little games that we play to open ourselves up. Constantly meditating, saying these mantras over and over again. That’s great, but if you can work it into the rest of your life, that is what shifts how you see things. It doesn’t immediately show up in your life. Like, your life gets better. Personal development is great, and you get those benefits, but it is doing something unseen. It is opening up some aperture and potential that you can’t see. And it takes a while.

There’s more of you

Steven: So I start to get some breakthrough experiences. I wanna remember, and I wanna wake up. And I start to. So I start to go through experiences where I wake up in profound states, and I realize that there is more to me specifically. To give you a short example, I’d had previews of these experiences before. Earlier, in one of my depressive states in, like, January, ’cause we’re now in late February in the timeline, just sort of beating myself up. It was supposed to be a meditation, ended up just being a whine fest.

About halfway through one of my thoughts, I hear my own voice. Like, hear it. You haven’t lived until you heard yourself talk to you. So a lot of people talked about having a higher self, and you know there’s more of you outside of this place, right? It doesn’t all fit in your human existence. You can call it heaven, you can call it the afterlife, you can call it other dimensions. I don’t care. Call it outside the simulation. Whatever your worldview is, there’s more of you than you are aware of here, right? And it’s doing stuff. For some of us, I think it’s asleep.

One day in February, late February, I wake up as this higher self, completely embodied in my current existence. That day is far too profound and weird to describe here. Okay. This is weird.”

And then a few days later, I have another experience where I feel like I am a field of awareness from a single point. I don’t have any of my memories. I wake up just like, “Whoa.” And I am completely unattached from my life. It’s like I’m being shown another perspective of self. I am the field of consciousness from the point of Steven. I’m no longer Steven. It’s almost, like, outside my own soul. It’s very weird. It’s a strange experience.

I think these are, like, Atman and Brahman experiences, because I get a follow-up experience right after that where I am this ray of light. This is after I wake up. I’m feeling these things. These are happening to me. I’m a ray of light that’s being shined through a bunch of fractal prisms, and every time it goes through the prism is like, this is, like, a conscious entity. It’s like a part of myself, and that at the end of the line where the light is shining, hitting, it’s like where I am now. But it’s already gone through all these other changes. But I’m all the light as well. So if you take the first experience, that’s me completely unfiltered, and then you shine it through all these lenses, and then you get me where I am here.

Really weird, really weird experiences, but they all feel like me. At no point in time do they feel like someone else. And I feel like I’m being shown different perspectives of, like, these are all true. You’re definitely not just this guy in this body. That is happening. But also check this out, and check this out, and check this out. These are also true, and they all felt like me.

And so these keep me motivated, and I know that stuff is happening. But it’s not easy. I still become depressive. After each of these experiences, there’s a comedown. I can’t repeat it, or I can’t do it on purpose. So many of these things are just happening to me, and it’s very frustrating. I’m getting insights, and lots of incredible things are happening, which we’ll cover in many other episodes. But I never lose the plot of this main thread. I want Reality 102. I wanna know what the heck is going on here. I wanna know who I really am. What’s going on? What do you mean, “see”? What am I supposed to see? What am I waking up from? Who am I? What am I remembering?

I never lose this plot, so I’m starting to get little pieces. They don’t fit together. There’s no cohesiveness. There’s no connection between all these experiences. But I mention these only because I’m getting results now. But they’re piecemeal, like a dart. There’s no pattern. I can’t find a narrative through it all to make sense of it. I don’t understand what it means. So I’ll pause there before we get to, you know, at least a partial reveal. ‘Cause it works.

It always feels like you

Joshua: Yeah, I’m wondering if all of these different experiences are continuously having their own experience. Are they aware of you? Are they also seeking integration? Steven: I think we’re gonna gloss over that part, because we’ve got so many damn selves. Joshua: Oh, no, I was just doing that gesture to kind of imagine what you’re explaining. So it’s like you’re a small self, your ego-based self. April 8th

Steven: Yeah. So all of that is crazy. It feels good. But I have never lost the main thread here. I wanna recapture that state that I had at the end of Reality 101. Why do I not feel like I have power and awareness? I’m going through all this stuff, but it just feels piecemeal. When do I get to feel like that? When do I get to know? What’s on the other side of this? Like, punching this stupid veil. What is this crap? I keep getting little pinpricks, like a little light coming through.

So one day, what is this? April 8th now. This is a long time. It’s been almost four and a half months since that experience. It’s been a slog. It’s been amazing, up and down, a lot of whiplash. And this entire experience is also documented on the blog. This one is called “See.” I sit down for a meditation, which is otherwise, honestly, a pretty crappy meditation. It ends up being an hour of just like a whine fest.

And I started doing this recently, but I remember praying, which is very unusual for me. So I’m former atheist. Now I’m anti-organized religion. I don’t know what you call it. I know. I know there’s stuff out there. I mean, look at the sign off of the show. “Everything loves you.” If that’s not spiritual and cosmic, I don’t know what is. So there we go. We worked it in. But I remember praying. Like, “Come on. Give this to me. I’ve been working so hard here. Come on. Give me something.”

And I start seeing these visuals. It’s the end of an hour meditation. I’m stone cold sober, man. I may as well not have meditated, right? It’s just a whine fest in the closet. Plus, you know, a little sincere praying. And I start to see some visuals. This is not completely unusual for me. Sometimes I get little visuals. I call it draw sight. It’s just images. We’ll talk about it when we get to mindsight and other kinds of stuff later on. I see some symbols, like, “Oh, that’s cool.”

And then they start to get really vivid. So I am seeing these. These are in the visual field. I’m sitting in my walk-in closet. It’s dark, but I can see them whether my eyes are open or closed. They’re being drawn into my visual field. And like, “Oh, that’s cool.” And then they get rainbow colored and extremely vivid. I’m like, “Okay, this is pretty weird,” because normally I got to be in a really altered state to get visuals like this. It’s pretty weird to have this happen to you when you’re just in a regular state. I’m a little whiny. I’m in a bad mood.

And I’m seeing all these geometric shapes, like rainbow colored bubble wrap. I’m zooming through planes and tesseracts, all these crazy shapes and spirals and just insane visuals. Like off the charts music visualization, rainbow visuals. Like, whoa. And this is so wild that I’m watching this for like an hour and a half, right? So I’m in the closet for almost three hours at this point. Because it’s amazing. Like, whoa. But it’s totally lost on me, man. I don’t know what the heck is happening. I’m watching this zooming and going across a black hole event horizon. There’s all these cones coming out and like, whoa.

And I just know, I have this profound sense that every single one of these impressions means something. It’s like sacred geometry. It all is describing some beautiful, amazing truth about the universe. If I could record this, I could sell it for 10 grand because I just know that math nerds out there would be like, “Whoa.” Just pouring over this. It’s just going down the drain for me because it’s totally wasted on me. I’m like, “This is some cool fireworks. I know it means something. Don’t know what.”

And all kinds of crazy stuff. I remember at one point I felt I was sitting in a cave. I’m sitting in my closet. And so I’m watching this happen. And there’s just this tidal, this gush of rainbow colored liquid that comes in through the cave, through the mouth of the cave. And I’m in the cave. And then it hits me. As it washes over me, I just feel this electric surge of power. It’s all information. Every single one of these little particles is just hyper dense information. I’m like, “Whoa.”

The hammock

Steven: So at some point I’m like, “Well, this is cool, but I’m pretty darn tired and I’m getting hungry. So I’m going to go make a sandwich.” So I go make a sandwich. And the visuals just kind of fade out, right? You stop paying attention to it. And it’s like, all right, those are gone now. I’m like, “Well, that was cool. Don’t know what that was about, but that was fun.” The weirdest part of it was the state I was in. I was not in some altered state or a liminal state or any sort of trance. It’s regular me in a kind of bad mood. Like, “That was cool. That probably meant something.”

So I eat my sandwich and I go sit in the hammock and I’m like, “You know what? I should probably get out my journal and draw some of those symbols while I can still remember them. Maybe somebody can look them up and tell me what it means.” So I sit down in the hammock and I’m like, “All right, which one should I draw?” So I started sketching and I’m like, “What am I doing? It’s just me. I can just set an intention.” “Hey, self, show me the most important one I need to see.” And then I see a symbol. Boy, do I see it. And it snaps into focus real quick. I’m like, “Whoa, that was fast. Okay, cool.”

So I’m sitting in the hammock and I color in the symbol and I’m looking at it. And as I stare at it, I just start to slip into this weird state, almost like in awe. So here’s the symbol. You guys already got the disclosure on this show. So, you know, whatever. You don’t want to watch this. You already know that you’re moving towards these sorts of states if you’re paying attention. So here we go. Here’s the symbol. It’s an eye with a boy in chains. So these bindings. It’s a red-eyed reptilian eye. Wrapped up in chains, like suspended over this giant eye. And I know the boy is me.

And as I’m staring at it, I utter aloud, “I am not the boy. I’m the chains.” It just comes out of me. Like, whoa. And then the moment I say that, my entire experience of, like, I’m this guy sitting in the hammock — I’ll just read the actual journal. And then my entire experience inverted. A self was the relief, the hollow spot at the center of me, which was everything else. There is only a boy-shaped hole in my center. I feel it. I did it. That’s what I am.

So I am literally sitting in this hammock, just kind of drawing a symbol. And I say these words out loud. And the moment I say them, like flipping a switch, I am all of my experience around me, instantly snapped into an extreme non-dual state. And I am not just, like, non-dual aware, like, “I kind of feel like I’m everything around me.” I can intimately tell the trees, the clouds, the rocks, the hammock, even me, the guy at the center, it’s all me. I have a sense of, like, it was made out of me. Exactly the same experience that I had, what I was being shown.

And I’m kind of freaking out. It’s pretty jarring for that to happen. At the same time, I’m watching a guy freak out. And I have all these impressions of his limbs, his body, his emotions, his thoughts. They’re just all there. But it’s so obviously not me. It’s just a thing that’s happening, in the same way a radio playing in a room or a movie on the wall is not you. It’s just happening. I am rather fond of this person, but it’s just like a car. It’s like a narrative. It’s just like a wrapper. And it’s so obvious that these are just a bunch of different impressions that I’ve stitched together and called myself. But I am everything around me. And it is incredibly jarring. And it’s all me.

And as I’m sitting there, there’s this sudden quiet. Now, normally, at this point in time, I’d gotten better at it. I can usually reach out to soul families, whatever, at least get something, at least a feeling. It is dead silent. Nothing. Bizarrely silent. The whole world feels quiet. Like everyone is just holding their breath. And I’m like, “What the hell is happening?” Because I still have thoughts, but I’m watching this guy freak out. It’s so weird. And I just know that this is an important moment. Anytime there’s silence on the set, I’m like, “Hmm, this is important. Hold on a second.”

This is how it really is

Steven: And as I’m sitting there, I just decide this is how it really is. This is actually how it is. I am my whole reality. I’m not this guy. I just thought I was. This is true. And the moment I decided that, I wasn’t just saying the words, I decided it. There’s this collective exhale. I can just feel it. And I can feel this sense of triumph and victory. And I’m like, “Okay, good.” Also relief. Like, “Okay, good. God, he got it. Okay. Just trying to make sure he got it.”

Because you can take psychedelics and get into a weird state, a non-dual state. You can realize, “Oh, I’m the universe.” But this was profound in that I had realized, not only had I worked towards this state on my own without any sort of influence from the outside, but I knew what it meant. I understood directly. “Oh, I’m just everything here. I’m the rocks. I’m the hammock.” And as I was holding the hammock in my hands, like, I got it right. “Okay, cool. You passed the test.” I knew that this was just one test, that there would be more.

But I was feeling it. It’s hard to describe how amazing it was to feel it while awake. Oh my God, it was real. Four and a half months later, it finally happened. Okay. That was not just some weird dream or psychedelic. I’m here, right now, feeling it. And I put my hand on the hammock and the hammock was no more or less me than the hand was. And I could feel it. When I was touching it, I could feel it. Like it was part of me. Not like it. It was. I knew. When this happens, you don’t have to guess. You just know.

And I could feel that it loved me. Like it was shocked. The stuff that it was made out of was like a part of me that had been separated for a long time, was like, “Oh, he doesn’t usually realize that we’re the same person.” And it was like this reunion. Like I’d come home. Like, “Oh, you know. You know now.” And it was just celebrating. It’s so weird.

And so for hours after that, it started to fade. I started to come back down, reassociate with my body a little bit. But I was just glowing. And when I would stop and just look at things like my desk or the bowl, that state would seep back partially. And I would begin to realize that the bowl was me. It was made out of me. I could feel it. And I would get this profound feeling of love and connection from a bowl on a desk. Because it knew and I knew that we were the same thing again. And it was extremely profound.

And I can now reclaim that state on purpose. It takes a little effort. I don’t walk around like that all the time. But once you’ve done it, you can do it again. And so this was a major milestone. And I knew what it finally meant to “see.” “Oh, this is what you meant.” In part, you meant for me to see that you’re not the person. You’re not a boy in the world. You’re the entire world with a boy shaped hole in the center. You thought that you were this thing walking around in the world, but it’s the exact opposite. You’re the whole world, man. You’re your whole world. You’re a whole reality.

This wasn’t like, “Oh, the rocks or the hammock or the bowl, you’re technically all the same thing in a conceptual way.” Like, “Oh, I feel it. Holy crap. We are the same thing. I can tell. I can feel you and you can feel me. And we’re the same person.” I couldn’t control it, but I was aware of it. And as I’ve said before, awareness precedes control.

So yeah, that was the finale there for the arc, is that I finally in a waking state got to see that I was in fact my whole reality. And now I know it is true on another level. It wasn’t something that was shown to me. Something that I did and can do again, in the same way that I can do mindsight. I can turn it on.

And it’s really the big reason behind doing the show. I want to get people to that point where they can feel it, because it is incredible to be able to look at something or touch it or just sit there and feel, “Oh my God, it’s me.” All of this stuff. When you’re eating food, you’re eating yourself. When you look at your computer, you’re looking at yourself. It’s all you. But not just being told that, but feeling it without a doubt. Like, “Oh, this is true. It’s happening right now. I can feel that it is.”

I want as many people who want to realize that to have that experience too. So there’s the finale for this one part of the curriculum. If you only get there, man, I highly recommend it to anybody.

Joshua: This is Reality 101. Until next time.

Steven: Nothing is impossible. You’re not going to die. And everything loves you.

See, Wake Up, Remember: Part Three

Episode 4

Jeff Cameron · before you read

The most conversational chapter in the book, and Joshua earns his place in it. His own practice points somewhere else, he admits he is jealous, and he asks the question the series needs someone to ask: why should anyone try for this while they are alive?

Steven’s answers are here: on aptitude, on other paths being valid, on why he wants everyone to have what he has. So is a claim worth marking for later, that each time somebody does one of these things it gets a little easier for everyone else.

It’s like your body is just everything you’re experiencing. It’s what it feels like directly. And I needed to have that in a waking state experience, and I wasn’t in a trance state. It just, bleh.

But I needed to experience that to understand the lesson that came next, because I was being shown low-level fundamental rules for reality so that I could share it with other people. There are many valid truths and perspectives, but I was getting this perspective, so the nuts and bolts low-level consciousness stuff, because it explained a lot of the other things or would help explain a lot of the other things, and it was very useful.

But one thing I would say to when you mentioned you wanted it too, I don’t have a perfect recipe, but for me, this comes at the tail end of five months of doggedly repeating over and over to myself in meditation throughout the day. Not just repeating, but believing, investing everything I had into, “This is really true.” Trying to get the belief to the same level to belief that when you set your foot down, it doesn’t fall through the ground. It’s what I was trying to do with “See, wake up, remember,” constantly. I was feeding this thing like crazy, and then it happened, and I think that that really is the recipe.

That path will look different for everybody else. The effects and maybe the realization will look different for everybody else. Maybe the insight that they need is different for everyone else. But I think this is a truth that anyone can get to. That is how I got there. I think it will work for anyone, because that’s the sort of underlying model, but let’s see. It’s a theory with a sample size of one right now.

The gradual way

This is just a game

Steven: One of the things that came to mind is how non-physical people, who are in these kinds of states all the time or can move around them freely, how they experience life, I don’t know for sure, but I would reason that my twin, who’s not physical, has spent most of her life, if not all of her life, that way. They’re just happening. You can create them, but you don’t have to have them. Even your body is just an expression of yourself. Even your surroundings is just an expression that you can switch up whenever you want to.

“Why are you so upset about this thing? Just dump that thought. You’re not having fun with it. Why are you… I don’t get it.” So that is just as much instruction as the direct experience, is seeing the contrast between us.

The ceiling goes up

Steven: I think that it’s weird to me that I can still think and feel emotion. I can still go back down, air quotes, “down” into being associated with my experience. The expanding gives you the optionality to be expanded and to have more awareness and control over your reality, but you don’t have to. You can still just be a dude chilling, playing a video game.

You don’t have to be burdened by this greater knowledge and awareness. You can put it on. You can scale up and down. It’s like the ceiling goes up. You can go back to your old level, but you don’t lose the ability to be expanded or to stop caring about things that don’t matter or to shift your experience of what’s actually happening around you. You get more, but it doesn’t end up being less. You can still be smaller if you want to. You don’t lose it. So there’s really nothing to lose, except for if you want the confinement for some reason. Awareness precedes control

Steven: The reason these experiences matter is that awareness always precedes control. Those people did those things intuitively as extreme outliers. But if we all start to change it, we all gain a measure of awareness. There’s no difference between a physical and a non-physical reality as far as I can tell. It is the same thing. It’s just a different rule set. We could change it if we all push on it. We all change what it means ’cause we have that control. The inner senses that we thought were weak were actually incredibly powerful. Intuition, belief, imagination, all of these things are actually very powerful.

But the world around us has been made heavy by those same forces. They have, like, a momentum. Flying is hard because we’ve spent a very long time believing none of this is possible. There’s this weight to everything. I think some of it is built in, too. I think that this is just a lower magic or heavier place, probably by design. It’s nice that way. But we’re kind of stuck in a hellish scenario now where we don’t know that we can do more. We can change it. We don’t have to.

And you can do it at the individual level, and it always starts with realizing you can, and then you do it directly. It’s very strange, but once you have awareness over how it works, directly, not just conceptually, you’re experiencing it, you can begin to control it. And it’s not a lot. I can move a piece of tinfoil without touching it. I can move it under glass without touching it. That’s pretty cool. I can see without eyes. That’s pretty cool. But anyone could do that, and these are, like, perception.

It’s obviously not super strong. I’m not lifting a car. I’m not seeing across the world in real time or into the past. I think all of those things are possible. There are no real rules. Most of the rules are soft rules, and we can change them, but everyone around me is acting like dampeners. It’s super hard.

You can change your own reality because you are your own reality. That’s what I was shown, and then it kept being true, and then I experienced it. The reason you can move a piece of tinfoil is ’cause you’re moving yourself. The reason you can see without eyes is because you’re seeing yourself. Like, of course you can. We are each our own entire reality, and they overlap in ways we’ll get around to, based on what I was shown anyway, from my understanding. And that’s why you can do it.

And you can alter your own reality however you want, but you are also overlapping everyone around you. And we’re all influencing each other, and we could do anything if only we enabled ourselves to do it. We work together on this.

Getting easier

You’re trying to wake it up for the first time. But some people have, and so they’ve chosen to come here, and I think even in the air quotes afterlife, non-physical world, I think that you are still in a, maybe in a canonical sort of heaven, or you’re in a place that you can understand.

That doesn’t mean that you’re thrown to the winds, “You can go anywhere you want interdimensionally, and you can go be part of a hive mind now. You can go do all sorts of crazy, visit DMT places.” I think that maybe some people can, but I think that it might look more familiar and happy. Because I think that’s what your individual consciousness is ready for. You’re not ready to handle being your own reality entirely and controlling everything at a low level. It’s a lot of responsibility to take that on, and some people just wanna chill with their friends and family and know they’re not dead.

That is my understanding, is that there’s something for everybody, and that you don’t necessarily stop developing just ’cause you physically died. But there’s a level of development that only really happens when you’re under pressure and you’re forced into this.

So that’s one reason, and the other is if you can already do those things, you have no reason to really be here other than personal adventure or whatever, and I think it’s to help people, is literally their very presence is to help people get out of this, collectively, humanity, to help their current situation while they’re here. You have to work while you’re here, within the system, right? You come in to work within the system, but you forget like everybody else does.

Those are two reasons, is that it sort of levels people up. You get to keep your progress. You don’t get to keep your stuff, but you get to keep your memories, your personal development, your life lessons, your capacities of awareness and conscious control over your reality. You get to keep all that when you die, and so that carries like a progression system through all of your lives or wherever you go. And so once you’ve reached these capacities, for the most part, you get to keep them. And so you can move on or do whatever you want or keep being a human being. So you get a choice.

But until you reach that point, you’re kind of on a guided path here just because the universe is pretty loving. Doesn’t just throw you to the wolves. You’re kept in a somewhat zoo-like environment until you can graduate out of it on your own. That’s my understanding. Just interacting with people, non-physical, my personal experiences, that’s what it seems to be like, is that you’re growing up, right? That’s what it’s like, and everyone grows up at their same rate or, you know, they’re 40 and they still haven’t bothered to get a job or a driver’s license. You’re like, “What the hell are you doing?” There’s plenty of those people too.

So I think those are all the reasons. So either you already know how to do everything and you showed up to help people or because it’s fun, or you don’t yet and you’re learning how, or maybe you never will. It’s up to you. So I think that’s the most succinct answer I can give based on my experience.
