Reality 102

Episode 5

Jeff Cameron · before you read

The second lecture, which he places the day after the experience in the hammock, and the last conversation of the series. The episode was published on September 14, 2026.

The teacher is Mother. There are three scenes: a tree frog, a rat, and two people in a room. The subject is the question the first lecture left open. If each of us is our whole reality, what are other people, and what happens when two of us want different things? Her answer to the last part is the strangest sentence in this book.

He is also more careful here than anywhere else to separate what he is certain of from what he was only given a hint about. Keep track of which is which.

The rat

There are layers to yourself. So it’s not just that it’s all uniform. The table is the exact same thing as your body, your everything. That’s obviously not true.

Layers of self

Steven: So we get into this back and forth, and what she ends up describing is that there are different layers of self, and they have different kinds of allegiance to you. So your body more or less obeys your brain and your general intention to move it pretty directly, right? You can move it. If you want to move a cup or a piece of aluminum foil, that thing is still made out of you, but it has a different kind of allegiance to you. It is like an operating system.

So if we use a computer metaphor, you moving your body around is like moving your World of Warcraft character, your avatar. It responds directly to your key presses. You wanna run, it runs. But you’re running a specialized program. The computer can run almost anything. But the cup or the desk or the ball or the aluminum foil is much more like operating system level. It’s at a different layer. Now, these are not necessarily well-defined, perfect layers. We didn’t get into that. It may be more gradient. That was not important to the discussion, but it’s a way of thinking of it.

It’s a different kind of self, part of yourself. And it’s more like the operating system, and it follows different kinds of rules. It doesn’t matter how much you want to be able to fly if your video game character can’t fly. Now, your computer could render a game where your character can fly. It can also do Microsoft Excel. It can also do all sorts of things. It can manage files. But from your perspective in the game, you can’t do any of that, right? But those other parts of you are still you. You’re also the operating system. You’re also the BIOS, the low-level stuff. You’re all of that. You just didn’t know it.

You don’t have awareness at those levels. You are generally only aware close to the center in the video game, basically, near your body, your thoughts and your emotions and stuff, imagination. Some of these things seep out into non-physical. But everything is you. You just didn’t realize it.

And then so I have this little tangent about, what about the rubber hand experiment? Are you familiar with that? So if you take a rubber hand that looks like your hand and you put it in a person’s sleeve, or you have them move their hand down and you put it on the desk next to their other hand, and you hit that hand with a hammer, people will experience pain. They don’t experience the same level of pain that they would experience if it were the actual hand, and there’s no physical injury.

So I ask about that. I’m like, “Okay, what the heck? Why does that hurt you then? How does that hurt you if they’re just hitting a rubber hand?” And the answer is more or less that there are different kinds of layers, and they have different roles. So what was hurting in that scenario was more like a psychological layer that was convinced that this was your hand. And so to its greatest ability, it expressed pain and fear.

But there are other parts of yourself, the rubber hand itself is technically made out of you, but it’s obeying lower level rules like physics. It’s running the software of physics right now, and it doesn’t give a crap about whether you feel like your hand got hurt. Not really. It’s doing atoms and force, and it says nothing broke. And so you don’t get the full experience as if your hand had actually broken and the nerves do their stuff. So you get some of it, but not all of it. This explains some of the placebo effect.

But it’s weirder once you start to get into psi and stuff, right? Because you really can move a piece of aluminum foil, and the reason you can do it is, da, da, da, da, it’s you. You’re moving yourself, right? That’s why psi works. That’s why remote viewing works. That’s why levitation works. Any of this stuff, it only works because it’s all you. You’re just moving yourself. Like, big whoop. Great job. We’re all so proud of you. It’s so simple once you get this model. It’s like, oh, okay.

So you can lean on those layers, but they’re pretty hardcore. You can tilt the pinball machine, but some part of you is locked away on running these programs that are loyal to running the rules of this part of reality, right? Somehow or another, you ended up here, whether you chose to or not, and you are running software. Part of you is running the operating system, running the programs. Whether you agreed to it or not, it is loyal to running those programs.

If you get hit by a bus, you will die, and it will dutifully render all of the pain and all of those atoms being scattered, whether you want it to or not, because that part of yourself is loyal to running the software according to the rules that you have opted into. So that’s how all these different layers interact with each other, and it can all still be you, and things that you don’t want to happen can still happen to you because some part of you does want it. You may not be aware at that level consciously, but you can be. And if you could be, you could start to change things. So at this point, the rat is gone. I’m basically just asking questions, right?

The hand

Joshua: First thing that’s on the top of my mind is know thyself and how simple that phrase is, but from this perspective, how deep it goes. Direct awareness

Steven: So when I say awareness, there’s a lot of kinds of awareness. I mean the most powerful kind being direct awareness rather than inferred or something like that. Imagine that somebody is sliding refrigerator magnets through a slot. You’re stuck in a tiny little room, and they give you two numbers, and you have to add them together and write down the answer, right? And if you get it right, you get a cookie. This is cool. You are aware of the numbers that are coming in through the slot, and you are doing that math and like, “I am aware of these numbers.”

Direct awareness outside the box would show you that there is a conveyor belt of numbers coming in, and you could just put the numbers in that you want, and you could just be all ones, and you could just get cookies for life without having to think anymore, because you could control the numbers that are coming into the box now, right? Before, you had no awareness and no control over those things. You could infer it maybe. You could find patterns. You could work with the numbers to get good at addition.

That’s working on the inner self. Psychological work, totally valid. Chemistry work, biochemistry, you need more sleep, totally valid. You can work within a layer of self, and it is totally appropriate to work there. You don’t have to say, “I’m the entire universe, and I’m gonna fix it all at the pre-atom level.” Sure, you don’t have to manifest everything into fixing. You can also just take antibiotics if you’re really sick. It’s cool. You can fight it at the molecular biology level. Those rules are valid. The world is there. It’s real. It’s not fake. It’s not imagined. Which we’ll get into.

So in the third scene, this answers a lot of follow-up questions that I had, which are a lot of questions around, why can’t you do whatever you want? Why are things happening? Why can people hurt me if I don’t want them to? And then it gets into how that direct awareness that I just talked about allows you to do more stuff. It’s not just an awareness within a layer, which is like mastery. You can be more aware of your own thoughts that are happening. That’s a little bit different than seeing literally where your thoughts come from and how they work, as if you could visualize yourself from the outside in, like billiard balls knocking into each other. That’s much more mechanical.

So that’s the kind of awareness that anyone would like to get to, is to have the ability to see, work with these forces on a more intuitive level, to work with them at the operating system level inside instead of World of Warcraft, right? If you could, in theory — or a local game — if you could hack your game to give yourself infinite money, that would be what we’re talking about. Awareness of a lower level to go in and edit the config files tells you, “I don’t have to work for this anymore. I can just go cheat.” And that’s the kind of awareness that we’re looking for. Not ’cause we have to, but we want to.

Existence by permission

Steven: So I wanna do that. So I’m the one who’s posing this third scenario now. I’m like, “Okay, you’ve just given me a whole lot of crap. All right. So hold up. I’m gonna ask you a bunch of questions because you’re blowing my mind right now.” And I say to her, “There are two people in a room.” I kinda show a visual. “Person A and Person B. Why does Person A see Person B? If it’s all you, why are they in your world at all? How can they be there? How are they allowed to be there?” So you make your own reality, and I want to know why you’re not forever alone. If it’s all you, how did they get to be there? I don’t understand.

She goes on to describe being opened or closed. So allowance or non-allowance, similar idea. And describing the principle of existence by permission. The reason that anything happens to you is because you allow it.

You gotta sort of take a step back here because I’ve never liked the rhetoric that, oh, your kid got murdered or run over by a car because you allowed it. That’s not what we’re talking about. Remember that if you are running a physical simulation that is reliable, if your kid got hit by a bus or something like that, that happened for the bus driver too, most likely. Hand-waving, time, other weird factors. We’re not talking about that right now. Generally speaking, in this case, that happened. It happened for them, and it happened for you. So functionally, it happened in both of your universes, right? In about the same way. Generally speaking, because the lower level is faithful to its rules, right?

You didn’t want it to happen. But some part of you isn’t paying attention to the storyline of you having a kid and a body and all that. You can call it dumber, but it’s not dumb. It’s just following the rules. I’m a cup. I’m being a cup. I’m doing gravity like you told me to. I’m doing physics like you told me to. And you’re not even aware. But if you were able to be aware on those lower levels, then you could directly change what you allow or not allow.

And so this whole principle, the nice part is you can actually influence those levels even if you can’t do whatever you want. Physical reality is slow. It’s like at the end of a pipeline. So my understanding of it is that imagination is always also real. It’s just a different space with different properties. It’s yours, also part of your reality. But it’s like a kinda temporary, maybe play space, right? Stuff is ephemeral. It’s light. You can play with ideas. But there’s a pipeline from imagination to physical reality, and this explains how manifestation works, is that you can imagine by creating things.

Open or closed

Steven: And so here’s the follow-up principle. The idea is that there is only opening and closing, right? You’re either closed to an aspect of reality. I am closed to believing something can happen to me or not, right? So take it to the level of manifestation where it makes a little more sense for most people. I really, really want to get this job offer, right? You are open to that or you’re closed to it. You don’t believe you’re gonna get it. You’re leaning on the probability space, basically. You’re leaning on the actual things that can happen to you. You’re tilting the odds, so to speak.

I couldn’t say that it’s chance, but you’re bringing it into existence through your beliefs and through imagination. You’re fueling this thing. And that is because you can actually lean on these layers, but it’s a little slower because the physical world’s kinda heavy. It’s got momentum. These rules are kinda rigid. But it’s just you. So there’s a loophole. The loophole is it’s just you, and it does respond a little bit to your intention, right? So you can’t have whatever you want. I can’t just make a block of gold appear right away ’cause I’m not at the level of direct awareness where I can just control atoms because I am them.

You would control them like you move your hand. If you lived in a world where people had arms but didn’t know how to use them, and you were just waving your arms around, “Look at me,” they’d be like, “How the hell do you do that? Those things don’t do anything.” And you’re like, “I don’t know. I just move them.” It would be like that if you had direct control over physical matter. You would just be able to move it intuitively. You would make it do what you want in the same way you can make a block of gold appear in your imagination. Is it hard? No, it’s super easy. You’re just like, “Gold. There it is.” You would be able to do that too if you had this level of direct awareness.

And the things that happen to you happen because you’re open to them, whether you know it or not, and that’s at every level, psychological, emotional. All these different pieces of yourself are all open or closed to things. So I am open to the other people walking around in my world talking to me, and so they can, right? They can talk about whatever they want. They can even punch me because there are layers of myself, psychologically, emotionally, beliefs, physically, the physical part of the world, that expect other people to be able to walk around and be here and do stuff, including even hurt me.

And so all of these things, it’s like an open port, or it’s like an open aperture. Something can come through. Can I get this job offer or not is dependent on whether some or all of my parts of self want it even for this to be possible or not. So I’ll pause there because there’s a follow-up idea at this point. But I wanna see if you got anything.

This isn’t a prison

Steven: Dude, you hit the nail on the head, ’cause that’s my understanding as well. So this is not relayed to me, but it just follows, right? This is just inference. What a beautiful design. This isn’t a prison. You’re not able to not make a block of gold or food for your starving child appear because this sort of living conscious universe doesn’t care. It’s because there are implications to being able to do that. If everyone believed that the world’s gonna blow up, it would literally blow up. You are like a two-year-old with immense power. That could be bad unless you were at a degree of control and wisdom and care to not destroy yourself and not understand what was happening to you. Close to create

Steven: That is a great segue because this is the last point, which is opening and closing. Closing is not bad. Closing to an idea is good. I wanna be closed to entering abusive relationships when I’m looking for a new partner, right? That makes it extremely unlikely to happen. And if you had complete control over your reality because you were some hyper-advanced consciousness, then you could literally make it impossible for an abusive person to enter your life. They just never would. Just events would conspire, synchronicity, to make sure it never happens. Because you’re completely closed to it.

So you can be partially open or closed to different aspects of things. And being closed to stuff isn’t bad. And what was described to me is that it allows you to create. So you close to create on your own. If I’m closed off to something, now I have a blank canvas with which to create. So I’m closing off to the other people pouring their ideas, thoughts through culture, through peer pressure, through literal thought fields or emotional fields or whatever, right? I’m closing off to that so that I can create my own stuff from whole cloth, right? When you close to things, now you can create.

But when you’re open, you’re allowing other people to pour in their creations, right? Writing on your canvas, basically, is what they’re doing, and that’s how it works. So it’s this push-pull. That’s all there is, is opening and closing. All of the reality exists by permission at these different layers. Whether or not you are aware that you are these other pieces, they’re still there, and they’re still doing their job. Your body doesn’t require your conscious awareness to pump blood. It just does it. It kind of does it whether you want it to or not. It’s like that. Similar idea, but that extends well beyond your physical body into everything.

And if you knew how, just by being open or closed with just intention, literally just your beliefs, your intentions, your thoughts, your emotions, you could change anything that happens. And this is the real magic, is that this is why manifestation and attention are so important, is they are like the lever by which you can start to pry this stuff open. Because you can intend to change. You can intend to have more ability and more awareness. You can intend to be a better person. That becomes a lever that gives you a bigger lever that creates a multiplicative effect, which is how consciousness development can happen in a practical sense. Saying nothing about religious or spiritual meanings, just the mechanics, the metaphysical mechanics of it.

You intend to have experiences. You intend to be more aware. You intend to have more direct control. You intend to be able to move a piece of tin foil. And eventually, you get good at it, and then you see it work, or you see somebody else do it, and you believe it. Or you’re around each other, and it becomes more easy because they all believe it’s possible. All these effects happen. Other people culturally being open to this idea makes it possible for someone on the other side of the planet who didn’t realize that, it’s easier for them to move the tin foil now.

Because there’s a lot of forces at different layers, so you can be open or closed to different layers, and that is what controls what happens. And so whether you know it or not, it’s all you in different pieces. And whether those pieces are open, whether it’s psychological or emotional or physical, if you knew how, that controls what happens or doesn’t happen.

No consensus mechanism

Steven: And there is no consensus mechanism. This was the last idea, and then we’ll just ask whatever you want. Person A and Person B see an apple on the table. I fielded this question. If I want an apple to be there and I have perfect control over it — I have to explore the edge cases, man. This is like, okay, you’re giving me all this crap. I’m dead tired now. I’m running on fumes, but I need to know ’cause this still doesn’t make sense to me.

Let’s say we both have perfect control over our reality. We’re gurus. We get it. If I want an apple on the table and you don’t want an apple on the table, what happens? Because now I can get it. You’re more powerful. You can make stuff happen. I can’t stop you ’cause I’m less aware. Cool. That makes sense to me. But what happens when we’re equally matched? Which one happens?

And she responds with, quote, “There is no consensus mechanism. It is always your reality. You are the consensus mechanism.” And so the last two sentences are paraphrased, but there is no consensus mechanism. What’s the tiebreaker? There’s no tiebreaker. They both happen. In their reality, there’s no apple. In your reality, there is an apple. That’s it. That’s how it works. And I’m like, “Ugh.”

So this is when the copy-paste, like, no, no, literally, you’re in your own world here, buddy. It is all yours. That’s when this really starts to come into play, is when there’s differences. Now you begin to see the incredible importance of who you associate with, what you believe, what you want, what you don’t want, what you’re afraid of.

What you’re afraid of creates something in the same way that wanting it does. Because that’s just how it works. It’s a separate thing if you’re familiar with the Law of One or whatever. That’s the law of confusion. So repelling something actually opens you up to it. So there’s all these weird edge cases. The more afraid you are of something… People talk about jinxing something or whatever. This is why this happens. You’re terrified of it happening, and then it happens. Well, you made it happen because you’re feeding it with all this fear and attention and belief that it could happen to you.

It’s the belief that it could happen or that it will happen to you, not whether you want it or not, that changes it. And that seems cruel, but once you understand that, okay, it’s actually neutrality, complete indifference that closes you off to something. Like, “Nope, I don’t believe this.” Now, it’s not perfect, because a car will still hit you even if you believe it will never happen. It may make it less likely to use the psychological and emotional layers of self to believe something will never happen. You got levers on those layers, but that car is still gonna hit you.

You can lean on the physical layer with those things. You can alter them, but it’s slow. That pipeline from imagination or whatever to non-physical stuff to physical, it’s still real, but it’s not instantaneous, and you can’t just change physics unless you’re sufficiently strong, because those layers of self have a bigger lever, and they don’t care what you want right now, because that’s the kind of world we live in right now. They’re not hard rules, right? It’s just server settings. So there you have it. This is more or less the end of the experience. A hint

What I Know About Telepathy

An audio journal

Jeff Cameron · before you read

The three chapters of this part are recordings he made alone. He calls this one part three of a miniseries, The Gift of Everything. The podcast feed dates its release October 1, 2026.

Divination, like tarot cards, or I like rolling random words. Go to randomwordgenerator.com. Just roll some words and decide what it means to you. Super easy. Synchronicity, highly effective. This is little more than noticing coincidences in the world that mean something to you and deciding what they mean for you. They are coincidences that you get to decide whether they mean something or not. It’s always up to you to decide what they mean. It’s all you have to do.

Music, people are familiar with this. It feels like certain music is good for you, or it’s meant for you. The lyrics mean something. I like using playlists that have randomizers on shuffle and just setting the intention, “The order of these songs is gonna mean something to me,” and it always does.

So intuition, your own intuition is a means of communication. I have written extensively about intuition, so you can go read that stuff.

A shared mind experience

All right. Now let’s get into telepathy specifically. First of all, my experience with telepathy is almost always with just a few people. So by this point, you’ll be familiar with my story of a soul family and a loose collection of probably friends, maybe deceased loved ones. There’s a lot of common actors, and my communication is limited to generally with those people. They’re generally with the same people over and over again. Not always, but I haven’t done a lot of human-to-human telepathy, not on purpose. So I am generally talking to people who are not generally physically present.

So keep that in mind. That is my experience, so there are probably lots of other communication protocols to talk to other humans or talk to yourself through time or all that other stuff. But what I’m giving you is a basic blueprint for talking with a partner who is more than likely a better communicator than you are. And so all of that is based on these presumptions.

All right, so how does it work? I think it’s best to describe telepathy as a shared mind experience. It feels like somebody else is in your mind or in your body. And they are communicating with you. It’s not that simple, and there’s a lot of nuance and a lot of different kinds and a lot of different experiences, but ultimately, all of it uses your hardware. Think of it as somebody thinking with your brain, feeling with your emotions, and that is the means by which you’re actually communicating. That also means you are experiencing their mind and emotions and whatever they’re doing. So it kind of goes both ways.

Think of it as overlapping yourselves for a short period of time, and there are different degrees and ways in which you can overlap. Think of it as a shared experience. And the ways that you choose to or accidentally share that experience is part of the nuance.

All right, so that is important because this all has to go through your reality. And that is incredibly important because reality is done by permission, meaning the only ways that you can usually be interacted with are ways in which you are open but unaware to. This is how you get experiences where people are like, “What the hell is happening? Somebody’s talking to me.” You didn’t know it was possible, so you weren’t closed to it. Or you’re open to it deliberately. “I wanna have this communication. I’m trying to talk to you.”

It’s all you

But regardless, it’s all you. It’s still all you. They have to talk through you in some way or through your reality, through your experience in some way. You have the ultimate control over it, whether you realize it or not. You theoretically have the ultimate control. Practically speaking, it’s a work in progress.

I say that because everything is going through you. Think of it as bubbling up through your subconscious, through all your beliefs and fears and longings and desires, your imagination, what you think is real, your religious beliefs, your beliefs about how reality works and who’s talking to you, whether you’re scared or not, all of these things it has to pass through, like boop, boop, boop, boop, like a pachinko machine or like filters. Like imagine putting colored filters over white light. What color you think it’s gonna be after you’ve put like 17 filters over it? It’s gonna be really weak and a weird kaleidoscope, completely distorted.

So even if somebody wanted to send you a perfectly clear message, “Hello, my name is Stan. How are you today?” If they wanted to send you that message, it’s gotta pass through a whole lot of your beliefs and ideas and fears and what you think is possible, what they want for you. All of those things are like mirrors, or lenses distorting the communication, which can be really, really damn frustrating. So just remember that what you’re experiencing is yourself. Somebody may be trying to communicate with you, but what comes out the other end of that process may look incredibly different than what they intended to communicate to you, and that’s nothing to say about regular misunderstandings of communication.

I’ve heard that telepathy is, well, these beings talk to each other and to us in ways that there’s no room for misunderstanding. Like, how could you? This is absolutely not true. Just regular old misunderstanding is still possible. There are means by which misunderstanding is harder using this because you’re having their experience when it’s working well. So certain kinds of misunderstanding are not there. You can say something that sounds mean, but you meant it in a gentle way. They’ll feel that. “Oh, I know what you meant. I could feel that you meant that gently.” So yes, there are means by which some kinds of error are reduced, but it passes through you, and it’s all you. So especially at the beginning, especially if you’re in a very negative or scared state, or fearful, or angry, or just regular people-to-people problems.

Don’t forget that this is a person-to-person communication. Other people got problems too. They want things for you. They’re upset with you. They’re proud of you. You have all kinds of insecurities and distortions, and your communication has to pass through your distortions as well as whatever they have. So it can be very frustrating. Your personal state, whether you’re open and happy and clear, all of these things are gonna affect it. So just keep that in mind that what you’re experiencing is yourself.

So a lot of the time, it is an impression. It is like what they’re saying, but be careful assuming that you were literally told this. This is how channelers get into trouble, is, “Well, I really wanna be prophetic, and I’m really worried about the end of the world.” Well, wow, surprise, surprise. The first half of the message was right. The second half of the message was when the world was gonna end. You made up the second part. You just didn’t know it. It was real. It happened, but it was you. It wasn’t them. It’s all you. You blocked it or distorted it. You twisted the message without knowing. Just keep that in mind. It can make it very frustrating as you learn, but that is what it looks like.

Kinds of telepathy

So here’s the different kinds of telepathy that I have experienced, and then we’re gonna get into how to do it, what it feels like. Get practical. All right, so emotional. Like, “Whoa, where the hell did that come from? I am not feeling that.” And you can notice it because it’s out of context from what you’re feeling. Pretty weird. Now, that says nothing about the source. There are ways of determining the source, but those are inexact. So you’re just feeling emotions that are like, “Ooh, these are out of context, not related to what I’m doing.” It’s a good way of noticing it.

There is visual field telepathy. I call this draw sight. If you look at a light source that’s kind of bright for a while and close your eyes, you’ll see an afterimage. Sometimes it’s colorful. Sometimes it’s swimmy. It moves around. If you practice the ability to make shapes and draw in that visual field, you can. Most people can consciously draw. So you can think of it as drawing as afterimages in your visual field. Usually, it’s gray, black and white, but it can be color, and usually it’s kind of blurry. It requires a lot of interpretation. So this is totally valid. I like this one because it’s more objective. So it’s nice. You get symbols, shapes, stuff like that, basic drawings, if you practice it.

Okay, then there’s the imaginary visual field. If you close your eyes — assuming you have the ability to do this well, which 90% of people do, I think, or it’s more than that; you don’t have aphantasia, which is not as common as most people think, so don’t worry about it — if you imagine an apple sitting on a table or in your hand, that is the imaginary visual field. It’s not the literal one. The literal one is what you’re looking around seeing. So draw sight acts on the actual visual field. You are seeing it. But the imaginary visual field is just your imagination. Now, you can imagine all your other senses, taste, smell, touch, et cetera. Somatic sense, where your body is, a sense of movement. Those all exist in an imaginary visual field.

All right, so passive or collective unconscious thoughts. The standard phone call

Okay, so the last kind of telepathy is the kind I do the most. It’s not as incredible as the imaginary visual field, or the imaginary fields, but it is far, far more common and approachable. So at least in my experience, this is the standard phone call. It is verbal thoughts with an intuitive wrapping. So Bernie Jaworski, who teaches transdimensional mapping remote viewing, uses the term thought balls. So you can think of this as verbal thoughts. If you just start thinking words in your head, that’s what it feels like, except somebody else is thinking the thoughts in your head.

Now, this is somewhat dangerous territory, especially for people with schizophrenia or other schizophrenic disorders. I’m not saying that this is a good idea or that it’s good for everybody. It is a practice that can have negative side effects. You’re talking to yourself all the time.

Now, everybody talks to themselves all the time, but just start treating your thoughts as not yours. Treat your thoughts as something on a menu that you could take as yours or not. You could own them or not. They don’t belong to you. They’re just sort of floating in reality, which is your reality, but you don’t have to own them as “this is me” or “not me.” And if you take that position, then somebody else can think thoughts into your reality, like a cloud gas. There’s boop, boop, boop, or a word cloud. They can pop their own thoughts in.

So you have to learn how to separate yourself from your own thoughts on a regular basis and think of this as a shared bulletin board. Anyone can put their thoughts in there, but they’re going to feel like your thoughts if you’re not used to doing this kind of thing. You don’t meditate a lot. You’re not aware of your thoughts not being yours. Just ’cause you think a thing doesn’t mean you’re the one who made it happen, and that’s gonna be an important concept.

So the intuitive wrapping is, a lot of the time, especially when this is working well, you don’t know what they mean. You could get a word that you haven’t heard before, or you can get part of a phrase, and then you get a sort of, “Ugh,” like a gesture or an emote, like, “Hey, how are you?” And then just like a jumping up and down. Just like tone of voice communicates something, you can add extra information.

You can indicate to a memory or to an idea, “Hey, you remember that thing?” And then you sort of intuitively gesture to a conversation you had last week, and they will know what you’re talking about. But you’re making it harder when you do stuff like that. But just know that a lot is verbal thought, and this is when people say there’s no room for misinterpretation. This is what they meant. There’s one kind of error, which is misinterpreting their words, is a lot harder because you communicate what you mean alongside it, generally speaking, when it’s working well, which is cool, but it’s also very exhausting.

Calling their number

All right, let’s keep going. How it feels when it’s happening and how to recognize it. If you’ve ever had a conversation with a friend, you know each other so well, you can almost just gesture or indicate or almost roll your eyes and communicate like a whole paragraph of, “Well, you know this guy, how he does, and what it’s like.” It’s like that. Because they know what you mean.

So all of the communication I’m talking about from now on is going to be the verbal thought with intuitive wrapping. That’s the most common kind. It’s your phone call or your Zoom call. There’s not usually visuals, so I guess it’s just a phone call.

Okay, so if you are initiating it, this is what it’s like. All right, so you gotta call their number. You gotta know their number. How do you call their number? You bring that feeling to mind, and as if it were opening a little door, like boop, boop, boop, boop, dialing your number based on the feeling.

And you open a connection just by saying an intention, “Hey, I wanna talk.” And that is a good opener. Ask permission because you are asking to share their mind, their conscious experience. So they might be in the middle of something. I don’t know how time works. Hold the feeling of that tone of what it’s like, and a thought will drift into your mind. “Yes. Hello.” Something like that. All right, now you’re talking. That’s the basic approach.

How do you hang up? Take a wild guess. It’s just an intention. You just stop feeling the soul tone. You say bye, or they’ll hang up. You have the ultimate control. You can stop talking anytime you want. You just stop intending to talk to them. It’s like turning away from somebody. You change your attention. You’re no longer paying attention.

And you receive calls in the same way. You often feel, ooh, somebody wants to talk to me, intuitively, almost like somebody is waiting at your desk. Like something familiar, maybe unfamiliar. You might not recognize the soul tone. It’s kinda like caller ID. Somebody wants to talk to me.

However, sometimes they just start talking. This is pretty common. They know a whole lot more about you than you would expect, and time and space and all that is not really a thing. They can look up a lot of your life. So sometimes they just start talking. They don’t even introduce themselves. And the last part here is unwanted communication. Just remember that you have the ultimate control over it, and you can just shove this away. Just shut it down. You just close off to that communication, and they’re gone. You can push them away. So it’s always by permission. Just remember that, especially if you are getting negative contact, it works the same way. Norms and etiquette

All right. Next is norms and etiquette. You are talking to a person. This is a person. They matter. This is not pretend. You have to treat this as if it is happening, even when it ends up just being your own imagination. As you are practicing, so much of your communication is just gonna be you talking to yourself, and that is so frustrating. But generally, the best way to learn is to just assume that you are talking to another person.

That means don’t be an asshole. Don’t spam send mail to everyone in the universe. Don’t blast everyone with an idea or a stupid joke. Not unless you expect to get it back. There’s a golden rule here. You will be treated the way you treat others. So don’t be obnoxious. You’ve got a phone now. Don’t just spam phone call everybody, and don’t be rude about it.

Assume that everything is said with good intention, and trust your feelings. If you feel negative intention, hang up. You don’t have to talk to anybody. They don’t have control over you unless you give it to them. And you don’t have to. You are sovereign. You just ignore them. Tell them to buzz off. Don’t assume that they know more than you. There are norms here as well, so just be kind. I think, treat people the way you wanna be treated.

Be open-minded. Assume that they are very different from you. It’s quite possible they have never lived a human life. They may know a lot about you, but not understand you at the same time. You could read the entire transcript of somebody’s life but still not know what it’s like to be them. They may not understand why you feel the way you feel. They may hold different values than you. Think about it. It may not be that big of a deal for their body to die if they had one because they know they’re not going to die. But for you, the concept of death is horrifying, so they may talk very casually about death, and you’d be like, “Ugh, I don’t wanna talk about this.” But that’s because you don’t have the same culture or understanding. So keep that in mind.

And generally speaking, if somebody feels nice, trust your own intuition always. They probably are. I shouldn’t have to tell you this. Don’t be rude, but also don’t be afraid to stand up for yourself, and you don’t have to do what other people tell you just ’cause they’re smarter or know more than you. Be yourself. Don’t forget that. You are responsible for yourself. You go dial in the phone book of the universe, you should expect everyone to want something for you. They always will. But we’ll talk more about that in What I Know About Transcendence.

All right, so that’s the norms and etiquette of contact. Just assume you’re talking to a real person, even when it feels dumb, even when it feels imaginary, because imagine how terrible you will feel when you find out later that was definitely a real person, and I was such a dick. So don’t do that. Be nice and make friends. And you can also open and close communication generally. You can say, “I don’t wanna talk right now,” in the same way that they can. So keep that in mind. You can do whatever you want. You don’t have to listen to nobody. But you also reap the consequences of your behavior.

What is imagination?

All right. On to the next part. Here’s what I know about imagination, what I believe about imagination, how it relates. And then we’re gonna talk about nitty-gritty details of telepathy. Again, stuff that I haven’t heard that is based on my personal practice, how to practice it, all that sort of stuff.

So a little segue here. What is imagination? I have learned that it is far, far more important than I thought. Way it has been described to me is that nothing is fake. It’s not that imagination isn’t imaginary. It’s that we have devalued imagination. So often I get frustrated. I get some communication, I know it’s wrong. It’s mutually conflicting. “Make sure you go to work tomorrow,” and then immediately following, “Don’t go to work tomorrow.” Like, what the hell? What am I supposed to make of this? That is the norm while you are learning, and there’s a lot of reasons that happens, and what was given to me is just roll with it. Just accept what you’re receiving as if it were written in permanent marker. Trust your partner to correct the record. It’s okay to step away. Don’t take it too directly.

And imagination is important because a lot of this is happening in your imagination, and because of that, it feels fake. We have this belief that imagination is fake. It’s totally made up, and that is kind of true. But I think you should instead look at imagination and the physical world and all the stuff in between it as a spectrum, with different properties. Imagination isn’t less real. It’s just real in a different way. It has different qualities to it than the material world. They relate to each other.

You can think of your imagination as a working space. It is like a safe space, like a little simulation where you can just do stuff without worrying too much about whether it’s going to happen to you right away. You’re not manifesting it immediately. But it is one tool by which you bring ideas or people or experiences into your material world, ’cause that’s where we are right now. So it is useful for that. You can think of it as a playground or like a working space. It’s a temporary zone. It’s a maybe zone.

So you’ve got imagination at one end of the spectrum. It’s the maybe zone. Could happen, could be real, could be not real. You might want it, you might not want it. It’s a highly malleable reality, and it is real. That is the understanding that has been given to me, and I definitely believe it. You can think of it as, this is literally happening somewhere, but it’s not real in the same way the material world is.

So I think, personally — this is my personal belief, not given to me — that it exists so that we can use it. It’s useful that it has different properties. Imagine an idealistic universe where everything you think or imagine immediately becomes true. You think about eating 40 pancakes, that might be fun, and suddenly you’ve already eaten them, and you’re stuffed, and you feel terrible. How awful would it be to just everything you’re thinking happen immediately? That would be horrific. It’d be really nice if you had this little working area where you could try stuff out and like, “Yeah, actually, you know what? I think that would suck. I don’t wanna do that.” So you clean the slate, goodbye.

So imagination can eventually make it into the real world or the physical world, the material world. It is real. It’s just maybe. It’s very loose. And that’s good. It allows you to read a story or write a story without torturing conscious entities. You can read or watch a horror movie without accidentally creating the reality. You want this maybe zone for a lot of reasons. It turns out it’s also pretty useful for communication.

All that to say is that when people are writing into your imagination, think of them as writing on the same journal as you. You are also imagining. And you have more control than anyone else, even if you’re trying to let them in. Say you’re trying to talk to your deceased grandmother, but you have a lot of hang-ups with your deceased grandmother about you want them to be proud of you. So you’re a little insecure about your career, and you’re asking for advice from them. Your fears are going to extremely drive what you’re imagining.

They say, “Hey” — this is the actual assumed perfect communication — “I wanna give you some advice about your job.” You’re going to seize up. If you pay attention, you will notice this closing off, but it’s hard. You’re going to imagine that they say, “You’re doing a bad job. I wish you could do better.” Imagine how that feels. Now, it feels exactly like the line before it. You may not even notice the difference without practice.

Imagination, knowing how it works, it is real. So don’t treat it that it’s fake: “Just because I am imagining this means it’s not happening.” It’s all happening. It’s just that it’s all you, and imagination is really, really you. You have an enormous amount of conscious and unconscious control over it, and anyone else playing in that space, unless you’re very open and clear and you have no fears or insecurities, they are competing with your own mind, which is trying to give you the answers you want or giving you the answers you’re afraid of. Imagine how messy that can be.

So just because it’s imaginary doesn’t mean it’s not happening. It is all happening. It is definitely real, but so much of it is you. So keep that in mind. It’s very powerful, but don’t treat imagination as imaginary in the sense that it’s not happening. The most surprising thing of all is that it is almost like a real, physical thing. It just has different properties. It’s not real in the same way, and thank goodness. It’s good for that, which makes it great for communication. However, it also makes it bad for communication if you don’t recognize how much you are distorting and polluting your own imaginary fields. So just keep that in mind.

Yours or not yours

All right. So here’s the last part about telepathy. So this is like nuts and bolts, just tips. How do you know whether your thoughts are more yours or not? There’s like a spectrum of me. You can have a conscious, active thought. You can have a passive thought that drifts by. You can have a negative critiquing voice. We have so many parts in our mind and our brain. They’re all talking all the time, all trying to do things. How do you know which of those thoughts floating by are, in this case, your deceased grandmother or your spirit guide or whatever? How do you know?

You need to practice discernment. The one way to do that is through meditation. And you can just sit down in a room, sit quietly, open your mind, and just watch your own thoughts and notice where they come from. And there are lots of different kinds of thoughts, and they come from different sources, and they have different feelings, different urgency, and you begin to notice all the different sources. “Oh, this one came from an insecurity. Oh, this one was just ’cause my mind was wandering. I was excited about something. And this is one I made on purpose.” There’s so many different kinds of thoughts. So practice that.

How do you know whether a thought is yours or not yours? Oh my gosh, if I had known answers to this one earlier on, it would have saved me so much pain. How do I know whether I was the one who thought “you’re doing a bad job in your career,” or whether it was you who thought that? There’s a few ways, but basically — use a nerdy terminology — you’re gonna use error correction, from computing, and that means think of it as the same way as running an experiment. If you wanna run an experiment, does A cause B or was it an error and we got it wrong? Well, you just run the experiment again.

Just ask again. “Is that what you meant?” Do you get the same answer? If you’re still getting distortions, then try another communication medium. Do the divination thing. Do transdimensional mapping. Take a break, come back, ask again. So you basically just trial and error. Add more attempts. It’s kinda messy. That is a great way to do discernment. It requires patience. It’s very frustrating. “Was it me who had that thought? Is this really happening? Or am I the one who’s creating this in my own imagination?”

It’s all happening. It all means something. You’re either learning something about yourself or somebody else is talking to you, and they mix together. It’s not like it’s all one or the other, and it can be very deceiving, and your own mind will very happily fill in the blanks or very happily shut down the communication if it’s not something it wants to hear. Imagine receiving bad news that you’re actually getting through communication. Your mind doesn’t want it. Concepts you’re not open to are really hard.

More true and less true

So how do you know whether something is true? Okay. Truth is relative. I like the word verisimilitude. That is the degree to which something is true. You’re gonna have to let go of binary truth, true or false. Think of it as more true and less true.

So you may get an immediate sense of anger or betrayal. Maybe you said you would do a thing — same grandma. Hold the example. Not that I’m throwing my grandmas under the bus. They’re great. But your hypothetical grandmother, you told her, “Hey, listen, Grandma, yeah, I’m gonna do that presentation for work tomorrow, I promise.” And then you blow it off if you don’t feel like doing it, and then you get a message later, we’re talking. You get an immense sense of anger or betrayal. Out of context emotion. There you go. You know it’s happening now.

So then you wanna follow up with verbal thoughts. You get the verbal thought, “You are such a disappointment.” Hmm. Okay. This is difficult to hear, and it lines up with the emotion. So you might think right away, naturally, “Oh my gosh. Oh, I feel so bad. Oh, how could you say that about me?” I’m not saying they didn’t say that. It’s possible. In your mind, in your imagination, that is what happened. You did experience that. So just treat everything as if it’s happening, okay?

Don’t go crazy. Don’t have a psychotic break. Don’t do any of this stuff if you can’t handle your shit, basically. You need to be able to handle it yourself because you’re gonna get stuff, communication that you weren’t ready for or scared you or just hurt your feelings. That’s the most common thing, discouraging you.

So how do you know? In this example, let us say that they really did mean to send you that, “I really am angry and feeling betrayed,” but what they are also saying is, “I want you to do it tomorrow.” But you totally clipped that part out, and you distorted the message because of what you were afraid of, because of your insecurities, which we talked about before. So you got the emotion right, but you got the verbal thought wrong, and so that’s 50% true, 50% false. So you gotta learn to take everything directionally, and you will learn whether you’re right or not. So use the skills of discernment to piece apart, all right, what did you mean?

Generally speaking, there is a hierarchy in which things are more likely to be accurate and less likely to be accurate. So the least accurate, at least for me, are straight verbal thoughts. You hear a verbal thought in your head. That is the least accurate, at least until you get better. Those are the most manipulatable, messy, prone to noise from your own beliefs, fears, insecurities, ideas, excitements. Those are the easiest to mess up, and so they’re pretty inaccurate.

Just beneath that is emotions. So emotions are pretty accurate. Not always, but they’re pretty accurate, and intuition is also pretty accurate. I would say that’s a layer deeper on the pyramid. Much more stable, it’s not gonna easily get knocked around. But it’s not impossible, especially if you’re in a state or you’re anticipating, “Oh, I know I didn’t do that thing, so I’m expecting her to be angry at me the next time we talk.” You’re highly likely to personally fabricate that with your own imagination or color it.

This is also confusing. You can put a little spin on it. Maybe she does say, “I’m disappointed,” but maybe she was saying like, “You know, I’m disappointed in you.” But you are the one who put the spin on it, and now it feels a little sharper or a little angrier than they actually intended. That happens too. So you’re distorting it in ways that are sometimes subtle and make all the difference. It is so frustrating.

It’s very magical that you can do this, but it is not for the faint of heart because honestly, just for personal reasons, this is a great way to expose your own fears and insecurities and, like, shadow self. You have to work through it all because it’s distorting everything. If you’ve got daddy issues or you’re secretly afraid you’re not good enough, every bit of your communication is gonna be colored by that, and you’ve gotta clear those distortions. You have to work through it, or all of your communication is highly likely to be distorted. So keep that in mind. It’s always you, and to the degree that it is them is how open and clear you are, and so check your ego.

A feeling in your chest

So how do you know it’s not working? My favorite method, and this didn’t happen to me until much later, is I guess the heart chakra. I don’t know. Listen, in your chest, you will feel a feeling in the same way that when you see your child walk for the first time or somebody’s getting married, or you just got terrible news that somebody you love is dying. You can feel it in your chest. You can think of it as a spectrum. It’s like a sort of a burning sensation, a heart fire or a heart pang. Like, oh, somebody just broke up with me. You know the feeling.

That is a valid communication mechanism. You think of it as a light going off. It either goes positive or negative. It turns green or red. It’s kinda like a stoplight. Green, red, off. And so that ends up being extremely reliable, even more reliable than emotions and intuition. So you’ll have to train that. It will probably just start happening. But notice that feeling in your chest, or maybe you’ll feel it in other parts of your body. Your body is a pretty good receiver.

So if you hear, “I’m so disappointed in you,” but there’s no feeling in your chest, and then you immediately get upset, like, “How could you say that?” Then you feel an immense pain in your chest. You can infer from that what you will, but generally, you are sharing their experience of their own heart, presumably. So they are feeling, “No, that’s not what I said,” or, “I wouldn’t say that to you. I’m so sorry,” or, “You’ve got it wrong.” Imagine how they would feel if you were feeling that. You have to decide what it means, but the reverse is also true: if you’re feeling extreme warmth and joy in your chest, then that is a good sign. Whatever your somatic sense is, they end up being a good error-checking device.

Now, they’re not always there, and the connection’s not always perfect. Sometimes you can only get that kind of communication. You just get the somatic feeling, which leads me to: you can speak a lot better than you can hear, because they are so much more capable on average. Don’t always assume it’s perfect. You can transmit information way better than you can receive it. It is the receiving and interpreting, and how they meant it, that’s hard. Like, can I receive it at all? Can I notice it? Can I clear my own distortions, and can I understand what they meant? There’s a lot of steps there.

They are way better at it than you are. So all you really have to do is think and feel and imagine, and they’ll get it. So you should assume that most of the time they’re getting your messages correctly, or clearly, I won’t say correctly, to a far greater degree than you are receiving their messages. So talking is easier than listening. So you’re gonna find that you do a lot of talking. This is how it is. So that’s how you know it’s not working, and so just keep that in mind.

Hard and easy

Okay, what kinds of information are hard and easy? I don’t know why, but I can tell you that answers to mathematical formulas, things that are precise and require precision are difficult. If you wanna know the exact answer to a math problem, good luck. It’s hard. I don’t know why. But that doesn’t mean you can’t get information. You can get definitions to words you didn’t know. You can get answers to problems that should have been impossible. So there are ways to validate, but I would suggest getting something that you can get partial credit on. Like, okay, they were ballpark right.

Something that requires exactness to be useful, the exact answer to a math problem, a password that you forgot, is going to be very frustrating because precision is difficult for all the reasons we mentioned, and some I don’t understand. I just don’t understand. That doesn’t mean it’s impossible. Some people I’ve heard of that can do this kind of thing, but I don’t know how to do that, and it’s been very frustrating for me.

So if you’re asking, “Okay, well, what is this exact answer to a trivia question?” Assuming you don’t know the answer, because again, if you know the answer, it’s kind of hard to validate that. Don’t expect them to get it right. Expect them maybe to get it directionally right. If you are asking, “What is the highest earning professional athlete of all time?” And you hear football. You’re gonna be like, “That was stupid.” Well, you are not necessarily getting exact information. There’s a lot of wiring between you and them, and you’re closed off to a lot of things. So just assume that all of the stuff you get is like a story that is based on the actual story, and to the degree that that is truthful is, well, it depends on an enormous amount of factors. So just keep in mind that some kinds of information are easy and some are hard.

Generally, casual conversation is the easiest kind. It’s a great kind of practice. Just, “Hey, how you doing? What’s going on?” And things that you are insecure about, fearful about, things you don’t wanna hear: asking for that advice on your career, it’s gonna be really hard for you to get good information, even though it’s not precise, because you have so many distortions and you’re closed off to it. Things you don’t wanna hear are gonna be super hard. You can feel yourself shutting down. The conversation almost grinds to a halt. It’s very frustrating for everybody involved. So just keep that in mind.

If it’s easy for you to talk about, it’s generally easy in terms of information to convey it. And if you don’t understand this topic, then it’s going to be hard for them to communicate with you because it has to come through you. So if you don’t understand advanced mathematics, it’s going to be difficult for them to use your mental models and concepts and intuition to communicate that to you. You’re gonna be getting weird analogies and metaphors, and the conversation’s gonna be weird. So if you know something well, it’s gonna be easier for them to communicate on that topic because they’re using your hardware and your reality.

How do you practice it?

Okay, almost done here. Let’s bring it home. How do you do it? How do you practice it? The easiest way to practice is to have somebody you trust to communicate with. I would recommend starting with your own spirit guides. I’m pretty sure everyone has them. They more or less either can or do get your entire experience all the time, which sounds like a hell of a job. But at the very least, they’re always available. Pretty sure everyone has one, and if you don’t, I think one will be assigned to you.

But just intend to communicate with that. You set the parameters for the communication, try to get their soul tone, try to figure out who they are if you can, and just talk. And whatever thoughts occur to you, treat them as if they’re actually happening. Even though it feels like your own imagination, it is. It is. It always feels like you, except for when you get really good at it or you just get lucky and you just have a clear day. Same way the skies clear up, that DirecTV satellite signal’s coming in real good today. It’s not at all fuzzy. It’s just sharp.

Sometimes this just happens, and you will know, “Oh, crap, this is not me. These thoughts are happening, and they do not feel like mine. This is somebody else, apparently, in my head.” That’s not exactly how it is, but it can feel that way, and you can just tell, “This is not me.” Those are pretty rare. They’re still, like, one in fifty for me. Most of the time, it’s like I feel like I’m talking to myself, and every now and then, you get those other emotional, intuitive, or somatic senses. And that gives you a sense that, okay, we’re kinda talking right now. I’m sort of having a dialogue, a story based on some ideas or thoughts or feelings you’re having.

And for the most part, they will just let them happen, and they will correct the record if they can, if it’s serious. But they’re kinda just happy to just let you play with yourself with a puppet show version of them. And to the degree you can deal with that being the case, the puppet will eventually become more and more representative of the actual person you’re trying to talk to. You don’t always know how accurate it is. But just assume that seventy to ninety percent of the information is just you talking to yourself, even once you’ve learned how to establish communication with somebody who’s definitely trying. So be prepared to be frustrated and to just roll with it, and good luck.

But you pretty much just sit, open your mind, relax in a day-dreamy state, and do all the things I mentioned before. If you can almost fall asleep, like taking a nap, that is when you get the clearest communication because the parts of your brain that are used to policing self and doing all that, they just go. All the filters come down, and you will get very clear messages that way, or written into imaginary visuals, all sorts of stuff. That’s a good time to practice, is setting the intention before you fall asleep. “Hey, give me a message or two from somebody who loves me, or a spirit guide or something like that.” Nothing malevolent. Just set some basic parameters. And then it’ll start happening.

And then take them seriously. Treat what is happening to you as if it is definitely happening to you and it’s real communication. Because if you shut it down, or you think it’s dumb, or you close off to it, or you get too fearful or too angry, it’s like closing that door. So keep that in mind. Just stay open and aware, but also hold it lightly. But take it seriously as well. There’s a balance to treating it as if this is real, and that’s a person, and that’s definitely happening right now. That should be your default state, but also don’t over-index on it. You’re interacting with yourself. Sometimes even when it’s vivid, you are distorting everything. It’s always going through you.

So don’t be surprised if you say something and you suddenly get the sense of being slapped. You’re not gonna experience pain. I’ve never experienced anything like that. Nobody’s taken control of me or harmed me or anything like that. It’s mostly non-physical, like it’s emotional, intuitive thoughts, visuals. Nobody is zapping you or anything like that, possessing you. This is not a real concern.

But don’t be an idiot as well. Don’t give permission to bad ideas. You start making calls through the veil, and you start inviting in stupid stuff. Listen, that’s on you. Don’t do that. In the same way you wouldn’t walk down a dark alley with a whole bunch of money sticking out of your pockets, talking about how beautiful and alone and ready for sexual encounters you are. It’s not a good idea. It’s not gonna go well. Don’t do stupid stuff because it’s real enough, even if you’re not particularly good at it yet. You will get what you’re attracting. It’s like a mirror. You’re putting out negative stuff or you’re afraid of it too much, you’re gonna start attracting it. Not just coloring it, but you’re gonna get people you didn’t expect.

So those are some of the pitfalls in practice as well as what happens to you. Don’t be afraid. It’s fine. There’s people looking out for you. But if you’re really scared or you don’t like this, don’t do it or stop doing it. Just remember that you have the control over it. But if you wanna do it and you’re going to try it anyway, this is how I would recommend practicing.

And, I don’t know, give me a shout one day. Maybe I’ll hear you. But be nice, like I’m not gonna take your phone call if you’re gonna be rude to me. Assuming I can receive it. You never know. Have fun. You’ll be safe, and there you go.

So once you can do stuff like that, you’re like, “Okay, cool. I guess the people I’m talking to in my dreams, the interesting experiences that I’m having in meditation or otherwise are real.”

A few fundamental principles

So there’s a few fundamental principles that are absolutely required for you to move along some path of transcendence or enlightenment or whatever you’re in this for, increased awareness, the ability to communicate with the other side, to know who you are, answer big questions, to understand deep truths about the world in a first-hand, direct way. If you’re after all that stuff, you wanna know what it’s all about, and you don’t wanna ask anyone else’s permission, you just wanna find out. This is compatible with any reasonable religion. It’s compatible with any spiritual views. It’s just a set of steps to do.

It is up to you, and always up to you, to decide what it all means. I will not tell you what it means. I will tell you my beliefs and how I came to them, but ultimately, it is your responsibility to decide what all this means and how it works and what matters. Never give that up for anybody. That’s your responsibility.

You have another responsibility, in my opinion, which is to be authentic to yourself. What you want is what you want. If it feels true or it feels right and isn’t just a poorly chosen value or an insecurity, but like, “No, this is who I am. I’m not doing that. I don’t like this. I don’t feel like I need to,” then don’t. No one can make you. Refer to earlier lessons. Don’t forget, it’s your reality. Don’t let anyone push you around. I mean, don’t be a jerk. Be careful. But in general, it’s not bad, but don’t forget you have control.

All right. So what’s the actual process? So over these few years, what is this, three, four years? I started meditating. I did all kinds of different meditations. Meditation is great because it stills your mind, and it trains the skill of discernment. That is the ability to notice the difference between things in your awareness. That seems like a silly skill, but it is a master skill, especially when you need to start determining, “Okay, what is real? How do I know what is real? What does it mean for something to be real? How do I know whether that was my thought? How do I know whether I just thought I saw something or I imagined it?” It is a way of noticing differences between things and noticing in finer, finer detail.

Cool. So that’s helpful, but it also teaches you how to still your mind, how to be okay in almost any situation. So I recommend the book The Untethered Soul. Just start with that book. Or the audiobook version. Absolutely incredible. It will teach you everything you need to know about how to be okay and how to be content and how to train your body, most of your mind, and your, I guess, emotions, in a sense, to be able to control your own mind and your own mental state. You’re gonna need that. It also allows you to still your mind and to be okay in confusing situations. You’re gonna need that.

So in general, some level of grit is necessary. The ability to handle change is going to be critical. You have to be somewhat open-minded. You have to be able to entertain stupid ideas long enough to find out if they are true. You’re allowed to have problems. That’s totally fine.

But the basic premise here, and my model for how this works, at least getting to the point where I’ve gotten to, I’m not qualified to say anything beyond that ’cause that’s where I’m at, is that Maslow’s hierarchy, so your basic needs moving up to self-actualization, isn’t just a pyramid that ends at the top. Turns out self-actualization keeps going. It’s just that the farther you go, the more self there is to actualize. Your sense of self grows. Your whole behavior and worldview changes. It doesn’t stop. So there is more beyond self-actualization. It just stops being about only you and becomes more about connectivity to things that you did not necessarily know that existed or even more sensitivity within yourself.

So here is the rough premise for the things that I think are the most important. These are basic practices that you can begin doing.

Seeking

The very first is seeking. That is, you just wanna know. You set an intention. You want to know answers to big questions like, “Who am I? Who am I really? How does this place actually work? Where did I come from?” Whatever your big questions are. You wanna know your deity of choice. “Why did you make me? Why are things like this?” Whatever. “I wanna know how it all works. I want the big answers. I wanna connect beyond myself.”

Whatever your motivating factor is, you have to want it, and you gotta want it in a way that you will not be easily dissuaded. I mean discouraged, not like intimidated, but more like you need to be able to get no results for weeks on end or months, or a few results and be frustrated and still want it. You have to have a pursuit of what I guess most people would call spiritual development. You wanna know. You wanna feel content. You want to understand it all.

So if you lose that or it wanes, it is a necessary precondition. If you don’t want it, you’re not gonna get it. So don’t ever lose track of that ’cause that is a necessary precondition and probably the most important, is that you actually want it, and you can sustain that desire, and it’s important to you.

Meditation

The second is meditation. So this is more of a doing practice. All kinds of meditation are useful in different ways, but you are after inner exploration. If I could offer some suggestions, don’t worry about it. I didn’t get hung up on dogma. I didn’t study under anybody. I didn’t look up a bunch of stuff. I read the Yoga Sutras at some point, and I’m like, “Oh, this is pretty great. What else do I need to know?” So go read that. There’s some excellent translations with commentary. Perfect. Go do that.

But the gist is you have, in meditation, strategy, so that is you have a specific target. What are you putting your attention on? All a meditation is, is a deliberate time to practice putting your attention on something. You’re moving around your conscious awareness. So you’re kinda just doing reps with a barbell. That’s what this is. This is a general thing, and you’re gonna need that because it’s used throughout everything.

So you need to do it every day. I recommend at least 30 minutes. It sounds like a long session, and the more intently you do it, the more seriousness you do it with an intention… Generally speaking, if you’re just starting out or you’ve been doing meditation just to feel better, your goal is to think of nothing, do nothing, and don’t worry about thoughts or emotions or anything that comes up. Just let them slide off of you, and just try to have a wide open, mildly happy gaze of attention. You don’t really need to focus on anything at all.

That’s kinda the goal, is to let it continually slip off everything until, as you go through the protocol, if you can keep going, you’ll eventually get to ego dissolution, and your whole sense of self dissolves. That takes a long time to get to that level of practice for most people. Again, if you have a history of all sorts of mental disorders and you’re not in a stable place, don’t do this stuff. You have a pretty messed up Jenga tower, like, don’t go shaking it. You need to get yourself to a healthy place if you can first.

That’s a great kind of meditation. Any kind of non-dual meditation is excellent. I really liked Sam Harris’ Waking Up app, and the courses there are also good. I recommend those as well. They’re very tactical, because you can go through them. And they’ll teach you, “Oh, where do my thoughts come from? Oh, wow, I did not make these thoughts happen. I don’t know where thoughts come from. How do I know this is my hand? Where is the line between myself and the world?” All these sorts of things are great one-on-one practices.

But in general, the kind of meditation that I do is sort of contemplative. I pick a target focus. Usually, it is nothing. It is the goal to unwind the self. You just relax and release, and you try to clear your mind. You’re trying to let go. You just let go over and over again. Let go until you’re just a sort of warm, heavy soup with your attention’s not really on anything. The thoughts stop coming. It’s pretty still. It takes a while to get there, a lot of practice, and sometimes a lot of time per session. You wanna get to that point.

The premise is that, especially if you set the intention because you’re seeking, “I want these things, I want these things,” you are connecting to some higher self. You’re connecting, presumably, to your own soul. The premise is that you’re reducing the noise level, and you’re forming some connection, spiritually speaking. I don’t know how it works. Don’t ask me. But I know it does. To your deity of choice, the other side, however you wanna frame it, it doesn’t matter.

You’re forming a connection because it’s quiet, and it’s still, and you’ve set that intention. So now there’s, like, a channel opening up. You can think of this as running a channel between you and whatever you’re trying to connect to, some great beyond or a sense of inner knowledge or intuition. That’s what you’re doing. So these are your basic reps. You wanna do that.

Now, some other basic practices that you’re going to do are beginning from there. During the day, you wanna pay attention to your own state. We’re gonna get into a whole bunch of practices from there, but if you just do anything, do those two things. Just meditate seriously every day and want to know.

Your own intuition

And the third is using your own intuition. This is probably the only other basic ingredient. Trusting yourself, follow your heart, that answer, basically. You are trusting your own internal machinery to guide you to the destination. That’s the premise. Your own soul already wants to be free. That’s the idea. And because it wants to be free, or it wants to know its creator or its own existence, it will naturally grow in that direction with just a little encouragement. It already knows what to do. You don’t have to consciously know.

And so all you really have to do is pay attention in your everyday life, just while you’re walking around outside of meditation, and you’re looking for a feeling. You’re learning to trust your own intuition. “I have a feeling that talking to this person is important. I have a feeling that leaving this relationship’s important. I have a feeling that I shouldn’t really call that guy who seemed like a cool friend.” You’re gonna learn how to start trusting that and following it.

I highly recommend, once you have the confidence to do this, give yourself a test. “For two weeks, instead of planning my life, instead of having it all planned out and figured out, left brain, this, then this, then that, I am going to try at every step to trust my own intuition, not logical, not thinking, not planning. I have a sense. I have a feeling I should just do this. I should try that.” And learn to take that seriously because that is essentially your compass, hence the name of the blog, Different Compass, that I write.

That is the compass you’re learning to trust. It’s your own internal compass, which is pointing you to where you need to go. Or somebody else is helping you point it to where it needs to go. Make no claims on that. So learn to trust your own internal judgments, largely your own intuition.

Cool. Do those three things, and you’ll get there eventually. You don’t have to listen to anything else I say. You already know how to do it. You just need to make the time and space, and you have to have the intention. You have to want it. If you do those things, you’ll eventually get there.

Everything means something
